Across binary occurrence prediction, 15-way retrofit-type classification, per-visit duration regression, and an aggregated monthly benchmark, classical tree ensembles remain the strongest standalone models. However, the LLM results reveal a consistent pattern: embeddings remain useful on tables (binary AUC = 0.982), direct prompting collapses once semantic signal is stripped by hashing (binary AUC = 0.500; multiclass weighted F1 = 0.018), and hybrid stacking yields the best manually built multiclass model (weighted F1 = 0.626). On the monthly benchmark, lag-based machine learning outperforms time-series foundation models, though Chronos-small remains competitive in zero-shot forecasting. The results suggest that on privacy-constrained industrial tables, LLMs are more effective as complementary components than as replacements for strong tabular baselines.

</details>


### [138] [Semantic DLM+: Improving Diffusion Language Models through Bias-variance Trade-off in Transition Kernel Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15327)
*Keyue Jiang,Yuxiang Wang,Yanan Zhao,Xiang Yu,Qifang Zhao,Bohan Tang,Baojian Zhou,Yanghua Xiao,Lin Qu,Xiaoxiao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过泛化误差分析揭示了扩散语言模型对转移核的敏感性，识别出渐近偏差、暴露偏差和优化方差三个关键因素。比较了掩码扩散和均匀扩散的权衡，并重新审视了语义扩散模型（SemDLM）作为中间方案。针对SemDLM的语义盆地问题，提出SemDLM+，通过全局转移和语义频率惩罚提升生成多样性和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型对转移核的选择高度敏感，设计不当的核会导致训练不稳定、收敛缓慢和采样偏差，但目前缺乏对这一敏感性的系统理论分析。

Method: 首先通过泛化误差分析，识别出影响性能的三个关键因素：渐近偏差、暴露偏差和优化方差。然后比较不同转移核（掩码扩散、均匀扩散），并基于理论权衡重新审视语义扩散模型（SemDLM）。针对SemDLM的语义盆地问题，提出SemDLM+，引入全局转移和语义频率惩罚。

Result: 在LM1B和OpenWebText数据集上，SemDLM+提升了训练动态，实现了具有竞争力的语言建模和生成质量，同时保持了满意的多样性。

Conclusion: 语义扩散模型可作为均匀扩散和掩码扩散之间的有效中间方案，所提出的SemDLM+通过缓解语义盆地问题，实现了更好的生成质量与多样性平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have demonstrated strong scaling capacity as alternatives to autoregressive language models. However, their performance is highly sensitive to the choice of transition kernels, and poorly designed kernels can lead to issues like training instability, slow convergence, and biased sampling. In this paper, we study this sensitivity through a principled analysis of generalization error and identify three critical factors: asymptotic bias (difficulty in approximating the posterior distribution), exposure bias (error propagation during sampling), and optimization variance induced by kernel dispersion. We further compare different transition kernels: masking diffusion yields sparse and easier posterior-approximation targets, while uniform diffusion provides stronger sampling-side repair but induces harder approximation. Motivated by this trade-off, we revisit a previously overlooked variant, semantic DLM (SemDLM), where the transition kernel corrupts tokens to neighborhoods that are semantically similar. Our theory suggests that SemDLM can serve as a plausible middle ground by reducing the posterior approximation difficulty of uniform diffusion while retaining repair ability. However, we find that SemDLM suffers from a semantic basin problem, where sampling repeatedly stays within a semantic region and produces low-diversity text. To address this, we propose SemDLM+, which adds a global transition and a semantic-frequency penalty during sampling. Experiments on LM1B and OpenWebText show that SemDLM+ improves training dynamics and achieves competitive language modeling and generation quality with satisfactory diversity.

</details>


### [139] [Probabilistic Signature Inversion: Learning Conditional Distributions from Truncated Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15332)
*Junoh Kang,Kiseop Lee,Bohyung Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将截断签名逆问题重构为概率问题，采用条件流匹配模型学习给定截断签名下的路径条件分布，并推导了线性统计下的贝叶斯最优误差作为理论基线，实验验证了该方法在合成数据与真实金融数据上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截断签名映射的非单射性导致路径恢复在结构上是病态的，因此需要将逆问题重新定义为概率问题，以量化不确定性并实现可行的估计。

Method: 采用签名条件流匹配模型估计路径的条件分布，推导线性统计下的贝叶斯最优误差闭式解（log-GBM）及数值解（log-fBM, OU），以此作为理论基线上界，并与截断签名条件估计进行对比。

Result: 线性统计下的经验重构误差与理论基线一致；截断签名条件的误差低于线性统计基线；生成路径能忠实恢复条件签名并保持分布与时间结构。

Conclusion: 建立了截断签名逆问题的良定义概率框架，估计器能良好校准到目标条件分布，并在真实金融数据上展示了超越参数过程族假设的适用性。

Abstract: The signature transform is a principled feature map for continuous-time paths, valued for its uniqueness and universality. Recovering a path from its truncated signature is, however, structurally ill-posed because the truncated signature map is not injective. We therefore reframe truncated signature inversion as a probabilistic problem -- learning the conditional distribution of a path given its truncated signature -- and adopt a signature-conditioned flow matching model as a practical estimator. This probabilistic formulation elucidates the fundamental difficulty of inversion: Bayes reconstruction error quantifies the irreducible uncertainty remaining after conditioning on a statistic. We derive the Bayes-optimal error under linear statistics, obtaining a closed form for log-GBM and numerically tractable formulas for log-fBM and OU, yielding a concrete theoretical baseline for model validation. This baseline upper-bounds the Bayes error under truncated-signature conditioning, since truncated signatures provide richer information than linear statistics. Experiments show that empirical reconstruction errors under linear-statistics conditioning faithfully align with the theory-derived baseline, while errors decrease when the statistic is replaced with truncated signatures. Moreover, generated paths faithfully recover the conditioning signature while preserving key distributional and temporal structures, indicating that the estimator is well-calibrated to the target conditional distribution. Together, these results establish a well-posed probabilistic framework for truncated-signature inversion, with applicability demonstrated on real financial data beyond the parametric process families covered by theory.

</details>


### [140] [DiRecT: Safe Diffusion-Based Planning via Receding-Horizon Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15359)
*Paolo Giaretta,Zeyang Li,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiRecT是一种无需训练的约束扩散采样算法，通过随机最优控制和滚动时域方法仅在最终干净轨迹上施加约束，避免了中间噪声样本过度约束问题，在安全规划任务中显著提升了安全性与任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在安全关键任务中通过将每个去噪步投影到可行集来保证安全，但约束仅定义在最终干净轨迹上，对中间噪声样本施加约束会过度约束采样动力学，严重降低样本质量。

Method: 提出DiRecT框架，基于随机最优控制将约束仅施加于最终干净样本；受模型预测控制启发，推导出滚动时域替代方案，将随机去噪与约束满足清晰分离，逐步引导样本走向可行最终轨迹。

Result: 在安全规划基准测试中，DiRecT相较于现有基于扩散的规划基线，显著提升了部署安全性和任务性能。

Conclusion: DiRecT通过仅约束最终干净轨迹的机制，有效避免了中间去噪动力学的过度约束，同时支持即插即用优化器、环境动力学先验和软奖励优化，为扩散模型在安全关键任务中的部署提供了高效灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful tools for planning and control by learning multimodal distributions over actions and trajectories. Yet reliable inference-time safety enforcement remains a key barrier to their deployment in safety-critical tasks. Existing approaches typically project each denoising iterate onto the feasible set, even though constraints are defined only on the final clean trajectory. Enforcing feasibility on noisy intermediate samples can therefore overconstrain the sampling dynamics, substantially degrading sample quality. To address this limitation, we introduce DiRecT (Diffusion-based planning via Receding-horizon denoising with Terminal constraints), a training-free algorithm for constrained sampling from diffusion models via stochastic optimal control (SOC). DiRecT enforces constraints only on the final clean sample, avoiding unnecessary restrictions on the intermediate denoising dynamics. Inspired by model predictive control, we derive a principled receding-horizon surrogate for the otherwise intractable constrained SOC formulation, yielding an efficient algorithm that cleanly separates stochastic denoising from constraint satisfaction, progressively steering samples toward feasible final trajectories without distorting the learned diffusion dynamics. Furthermore, DiRecT is highly flexible: it can leverage off-the-shelf or domain-specific optimizers, incorporate priors over environment dynamics, and optimize additional soft rewards. Extensive experiments on safe planning benchmarks demonstrate that DiRecT substantially improves deployment safety and task performance over existing diffusion-based planning baselines.

</details>


### [141] [Repeated Bilateral Trade: The Quest for Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15369)
*François Bachoc,Roberto Colomboni,Emilie Kaufmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从公平性角度研究重复双边贸易，提出Rawls-to-Nash公平增益目标函数族，并刻画了该族目标下的最优学习率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双边贸易研究仅最大化贸易收益（GFT），忽视了买卖双方之间的公平分配。本文旨在引入公平性考量，设计兼顾效率与公平的贸易机制。

Method: 通过非正Hölder均值聚合买卖双方净收益，构建Rawls-to-Nash参数化公平增益目标族；利用二维奇异核积分恒等式从阈值反馈中恢复期望奖励；在买卖双方估值序列独立同分布但边际分布未知的假设下，推导固定置信度样本复杂度与遗憾界。

Result: 刻画了Rawls-to-Nash公平增益目标族的最优学习率，给出了匹配的固定置信度样本复杂度与遗憾界（至多多项式对数因子）。

Conclusion: 所提出的公平增益目标族在理论上可学习，且学习率最优，为平台设计公平性导向的双边贸易机制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study repeated bilateral trade from a fairness perspective. At each round, a fresh seller-buyer pair arrives, and the platform posts a price before observing the traders' valuations. Trade occurs only if both agents accept the price. Rather than maximizing only the gain from trade, we consider platforms that seek balanced divisions of the generated surplus. We show that natural fairness desiderata lead to a one-parameter Rawls-to-Nash family of fair-gain objectives, obtained by aggregating the seller's and buyer's net gains through nonpositive Hölder means. Unlike the standard gain-from-trade objective and the Rawlsian fair-gain objective studied in prior work, our proposed objectives induce a new statistical structure in which expected rewards are recovered from threshold feedback through a two-dimensional singular-kernel integral identity. This leads to a nonstandard pure-exploration problem whose natural estimators are rectangular double sums with row-column dependence and singular weights. Assuming independent i.i.d. seller and buyer valuation sequences with arbitrary unknown marginals, we characterize the optimal learning rates for the whole Rawls-to-Nash family of fair-gain objectives, giving matching fixed-confidence sample-complexity and regret bounds up to polylogarithmic factors.

</details>


### [142] [Learning Earthquake Wave Arrival Time Picking from Labels with Inaccuracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15377)
*Sen Li,Xu Yang,S. Mostafa Mousavi,Anye Cao,Keting Fan,Yaoqi Liu,Changbin Wang,Qiang Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LaNCoR方法，通过对比学习对齐波形特征与标签表示分布，有效处理地震信号处理中的噪声标签问题，在P波初至拾取任务上性能提升最高达28.8%，无需大规模训练集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签噪声会破坏监督学习模型的性能，而现有地震学方法依赖大规模训练集或数据增强来缓解这一问题，成本高昂且耗时。因此需要一种能有效处理噪声标签且不依赖大规模数据的方法。

Method: 提出Label Noise-Contrastive Robust Learning (LaNCoR)方法，通过对比学习在特征空间中对齐输入波形特征与标签表示分布，从而纠正错误标签并降低其负面影响。

Result: 在微地震数据的P波初至拾取任务上，使用两种基线模型和训练方法进行验证，LaNCoR在各项性能指标上最高提升28.8%。

Conclusion: LaNCoR方法能有效处理地震信号处理中的标签噪声问题，无需大规模训练数据集，在地震学及地球科学模型训练中具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Inaccurately labeled training data, or "label noise", poses a significant threat to the integrity of supervised machine learning models. This corruption directly degrades performance by teaching the model erroneous mappings between features and labels, which leads to poor generalization and reduced accuracy on properly labeled validation and test data. Current seismological applications mainly rely on large-scale training sets or data augmentation to reduce the label-noise impact, which can be labor-intensive and costly. Here, we introduce a Label Noise-Contrastive Robust Learning (LaNCoR) approach that can effectively handle noisy labels in seismic signal processing tasks, without requiring large-scale training datasets. In this approach, the input waveform feature and label representation distributions are aligned in the feature space to correct mislabeling and reduce its impact on the training process. We present LaNCoR's performance on the task of P-phase arrival-time picking of real microseismic data using two baseline models and training approaches. Our results indicate that LaNCoR can improve performance by up to 28.8% across performance metrics. This approach holds great promise for model training in seismology and geosciences.

</details>


### [143] [A Compositional Framework for Open-ended Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15386)
*Ida Momennejad,Roberta Raileanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出开放智能的形式化框架，以有限原始集合P与组合算子C的闭包来定义，通过原始基元（表征与算法）与组合语法（递归、排序等）两大支柱，实现跨任务与世界的无限适应性响应生成。提出下一原始预测（Next Primitive Prediction）作为新型架构目标，结合课程学习与自我对弈实现闭包的生命周期扩展。在物理、演化与神经科学三个领域进行案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放智能（Open-ended intelligence）缺乏统一形式化框架，现有方法难以系统刻画智能体在全新问题与环境中的适应能力，亟需一种数学语言来支撑跨学科研究与架构设计。

Method: 通过有限原始集P与组合算子C定义智能的闭包\u2124(P,C)；提出两大支柱（表征/算法原始基元与组合语法）；设计"下一原始预测"作为训练目标；引入课程学习与自我对弈实现闭包生命周期扩展。

Result: 形式化刻画了支持无限组合生成的任务/世界族闭包性质；给出可解释性与评估指标的理论支撑；提出新型架构目标（下一原始预测）；在物理、演化、神经科学三个案例中验证框架有效性。

Conclusion: 开放智能可通过原始基元与组合语法的闭包操作来形式化，该数学框架支持跨学科研究议程，为构建具有原生组合泛化能力的架构提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Open-ended intelligence is the capacity to adapt to novel problems and environments that are substantially different from those in training. We formalize open-ended intelligence as the closure induced by a finite primitive set \(P\) and a set of composition operators \(C\). We characterize properties of the induced closure \(\mathcal{L}(P,C)\) that support unbounded compositional generation across families of tasks and worlds. A mathematics of open-ended intelligence requires two pillars: a minimal set of representational primitives (e.g., states, actions) and algorithmic primitives (e.g., nearest neighbor), together with composition motifs (e.g., recursion, sequencing) that reflect an acquired compositional grammar. The closure of these two pillars enables the generation of infinite adaptive responses across a wide range of settings. The mathematics supports complementary research agendas, including evaluation metrics for explanation and interpretability, as well as building architectures where compositional generalization is native. We propose next primitive prediction as a novel architectural objective, where the training objective encourages the acquisition of reusable algorithmic primitives and their compositional grammar, such that new solutions are generated through recombination. Curriculum learning and self-play enable lifelong learning and expansion of the closure by discovering reusable primitives and transition motifs across families of tasks and worlds. We ground the framework through case studies in physics, evolution, and neuroscience.

</details>


### [144] [Constitutional Value Potentials: reading and steering internal priority margins in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15420)
*Tong Che,Rui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出宪法价值势能（CVP）方法，通过从语言模型内部激活中读取结构化边缘信号，来检测模型在价值冲突中实际牺牲了哪个价值，而非仅依赖输出行为判断。该方法能高精度预测价值冲突违规（AUROC达0.95），泛化到未见冲突，并支持干预测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅通过模型输出判断其对宪法的遵循程度，但输出证据在价值冲突时最不可靠——模型提及何种价值不重要，重要的是它愿意牺牲哪个。因此需要一种能从模型内部状态直接读取价值优先级仲裁信号的方法。

Method: 为每个价值从隐藏状态学习一个标量势能（内部压力），通过独立评判器对模型实际输出所保留价值的判决进行监督。两个势能的符号差构成优先级边缘。宪法条款对应于边缘保持正值的声明，单个监控分数标记边缘为负的情况。信号从提示尾部和首个响应token出现时即可读取。

Result: 该监控器预测价值冲突违规的AUROC高达0.95，优于强隐藏状态探针，并在三个Qwen2.5规模上泛化到未见合成冲突。该信号还能揭示对抗性优先级攻击是否实际推动模型走向违规，而不仅仅是判断提示是否显得对抗性。同一方向支持干预测试：沿价值方向移动可改变模型权衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，部分宪法相关的优先级可以作为激活空间中的边缘信号被访问，而非仅通过输出行为体现。这为模型价值对齐的内部监控和干预提供了新途径。

Abstract: A constitution tells a language model what to value, but little tells us whether it does. Adherence is judged from outputs, and output evidence is most fragile on value conflicts, where what matters is not which value a model mentions but which one it is willing to sacrifice. We provide evidence that this arbitration can be read from activations in a structured margin readout. We introduce Constitutional Value Potentials (CVP). For each value we learn a scalar potential from the hidden state: an internal pressure to preserve that value, supervised not by the prompt but by an independent judge's verdict on which value the model's own response actually preserved. The signed difference of two potentials is a priority margin. A constitutional clause becomes the claim that a margin stays positive, and a single monitor score flags when it does not. The monitor predicts conflict violations with AUROC up to 0.95, beats a strong hidden-state probe, and generalizes to held-out synthetic conflicts across three Qwen2.5 scales. The signal appears as the answer begins, from the prompt tail and first response token. Read this early, the same signal reveals whether an adversarial priority hack has actually pushed the model toward a violation, rather than only whether the prompt looks adversarial. The same directions also support intervention tests: under selected steering settings, moving along a value direction shifts judged trade-offs in the intended direction. Together, these results suggest that some constitution-relevant priorities are accessible as activation-space margins, rather than only as output behavior.

</details>


### [145] [Post-Launch Capability Expansion of Vision-Language Models via Prompting for On-Orbit Spacecraft Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15427)
*Nicholas A. Welsh,Lennon J. Shikhman,Monty Nehru Attazs,Seemanthini K. Putane,Van Minh Nguyen,Ryan T. White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在轨航天器检测系统中，使用冻结权重的提示驱动视觉-语言模型（SAM3）实现发射后语义扩展的可行性。在129张未见卫星图像的测试集上，零样本实例分割达到0.385 mAP@0.5，大型结构（如航天器主体、太阳翼）定位可靠，而小型部件（如天线、推进器）表现较差。结构化提示比简短类别名提示性能提升高达82%，模型可在嵌入式GPU上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器在轨检测系统通常在发射前部署感知模型，发射后更新模型权重或扩展固定标签集在操作上不可行。虽然可以在飞行前集成监督模型，但增加新的语义能力需要重新训练和重新上传参数。本文探索提示驱动视觉-语言模型能否实现发射后语义扩展。

Method: 采用严格冻结权重的单次推理协议，使用SAM3模型进行零样本实例分割。测试集包含129张先前未见卫星的图像。采用固定全局阈值，无后处理。实验比较了不同提示形式（短类别名提示与结构化空间几何描述提示）对性能的影响。

Result: SAM3实现0.385 mAP@0.5和0.267 mAP@0.5:0.95。大型结构如航天器主体（0.639 AP@0.5）和太阳翼（0.598 AP@0.5）定位可靠；小型部件如天线（0.221 AP@0.5）和推进器（0.081 AP@0.5）困难。结构化提示相比简短类别名提示性能提升高达82%。模型在当代嵌入式GPU内存和计算范围内运行。

Conclusion: 提示驱动视觉-语言模型可为在轨航天器主要结构的发射后语义扩展提供实用机制，但零样本定位在轨道域偏移下对精细尺度组件存在局限性。

Abstract: Spaceborne inspection systems often deploy perception models prior to launch, after which updating model weights or expanding fixed label sets becomes operationally impractical. While supervised models can be integrated pre-flight, adding new semantic capabilities in orbit requires retraining and re-uploading parameters. We investigate whether prompt-driven vision--language models can enable post-launch semantic expansion, allowing new spacecraft components to be specified via natural-language prompts without modifying onboard weights. We evaluate zero-shot instance segmentation of spacecraft components under a strictly frozen, single-pass inference protocol on a test set of $129$ images of previously unseen satellites. Under fixed global thresholds and no post-processing, SAM3 achieves $0.385$ mAP@$0.5$ and $0.267$ mAP@$0.5{:}0.95$. Performance is strongly scale-dependent: large structural elements like spacecraft bodies ($0.639$ AP@$0.50$) and solar arrays ($0.598$ AP@$0.5$) localize reliably, while relatively small appendages like antennas ($0.221$ AP@$0.5$) and thrusters ($0.081$ AP@$0.5$) remain difficult. Prompt formulation influences performance, with structured prompts incorporating spatial and geometric descriptors yielding up to $82%$ improvement over short category-name prompts. The model operates within the memory and compute envelope of contemporary embedded GPUs, suggesting prompt-driven grounding can provide a practical mechanism for post-launch semantic extension of dominant spacecraft structures while highlighting limitations of zero-shot localization for fine-scale components under orbital domain shift.

</details>


### [146] [Beyond Classification: A Cough Regression Benchmark for Respiratory Acoustic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15436)
*Mayur Sanap,Prasanna Desikan,Edgar Lobaton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了五种呼吸声学基础模型在咳嗽音频上连续健康指标（年龄、BMI、疾病概率）的回归预测能力，发现MLP-small头部在大部分任务中优于线性探测，HeAR在年龄回归上表现最佳，但存在预训练数据重叠问题；揭示了数据集大小与模型头部容量的权衡，以及跨数据集迁移的强不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 呼吸声学基础模型在咳嗽分类任务上表现出色，但从咳嗽音频中预测连续健康指标（如年龄、BMI、疾病概率）的能力尚未被探索，而这些指标在无法获取物理测量值的场景中具有临床价值。

Method: 构建多模型、多目标咳嗽回归基准，评估五种基础模型（OPERA-CT、OPERA-CE、OPERA-GT、HeAR、M2D+Resp）在三个数据集上的六个回归目标，采用受试者不重叠协议，比较线性、MLP-small和全MLP三种回归头部。

Result: MLP-small在30个模型×任务组合中23个优于线性探测；HeAR在Coswara年龄回归上达到最佳（MAE 9.12年），但其CIDRZ结果因可能的预训练重叠被排除；OPERA-GT在年龄回归上全面优于OPERA-CT；HeAR和M2D+Resp在N=50样本时接近饱和性能，而OPERA模型需要N=400；跨数据集迁移呈现强不对称性。

Conclusion: 咳嗽音频可有效预测连续健康指标，但需注意模型选择、数据集大小与头部容量的权衡，以及跨数据集迁移的不对称性；生成式预训练（OPERA-GT）相比对比式预训练（OPERA-CT）在年龄回归上具有优势。

Abstract: Respiratory acoustic foundation models (FMs) excel at cough classification, yet their ability to predict continuous health quantities from cough audio remains largely unexplored, despite the clinical value of passive age, BMI, and disease probability estimation in settings where physical measurements are unavailable. We introduce the multi-model, multi-target cough regression benchmark evaluating five FMs (OPERA-CT, OPERA-CE, OPERA-GT, HeAR, M2D+Resp) across six targets on three datasets under subject-disjoint protocols, comparing linear, MLP-small, and full MLP regression heads. MLP-small beats the mean-predictor baseline on all tasks and linear probing in 23 of 30 model x task cases, with full MLP overfitting on small clinical data but recovering on larger sets, revealing a dataset size x head-capacity trade-off. HeAR leads within-dataset age regression on Coswara (9.12 yr MAE); its CIDRZ result is excluded from headline claims owing to possible HeAR-CIDRZ pretraining overlap. OPERA-GT is favored over OPERA-CT on age in all three datasets, with the CIDRZ margin within seed variance, extending a generative-pretraining advantage from breath to cough. HeAR and M2D+Resp reach near-full performance at N = 50 samples while OPERA models require N = 400. Cross-dataset transfer is strongly asymmetric as large diverse data generalises to small clinical populations (CoughVID to CIDRZ: -0.17 yr) but not vice versa (CIDRZ to Coswara: +2.43 yr, +26.6%).

</details>


### [147] [PHINN: Persistent Homology Inspired Neural Network for Rare-Event Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15452)
*Emre Yusuf,Ren Takahashi,Jayabrata Bhaduri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PHINN，一种利用持续同伦（Betti曲线）作为条件信号的流匹配框架，用于生成包含罕见事件的时间序列。该方法在多个基准上显著提升了拓扑保真度，并支持多变量数据、自然语言接口、跨域元学习和对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列中的罕见事件对建模至关重要，但由于数据稀疏性难以学习，现有生成模型在极值处理上表现不佳。作者观察到罕见事件留下独特的拓扑指纹（点云嵌入中Betti数的转变），比统计矩更稳定且更具判别性，因此利用拓扑信息来改善生成质量。

Method: 提出PHINN框架：1) 使用动态Betti曲线作为流匹配的条件信号；2) 引入持续景观损失（persistence landscape loss）确保同调一致性；3) 支持多变量数据扩展；4) 提供自然语言接口设置Betti目标；5) 支持跨域元学习和少样本生成；6) 具备经认证的对抗鲁棒性。

Result: 在金融、流行病学和多模态基准上，PHINN在拓扑保真度方面显著优于统计基线和扩散基线：β-RMSE降低41-63%，转变准确率提高84%。在尾部覆盖上与跳扩散模型持平，且在形状保真度上超越它们。所有结果均具有95%置信区间。

Conclusion: 利用拓扑信号（Betti曲线）作为条件能有效改善罕见事件时间序列的生成质量，PHINN在保拓扑和保形状方面均优于现有方法，同时具备跨域泛化能力和对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: Rare events in time series are critical to model but hard to learn due to data scarcity. Current generative models struggle with extreme values. We observe that rare events leave distinct topological fingerprints - transitions in Betti numbers from point-cloud embeddings - that are more stable and discriminative than statistical moments.
  We introduce PHINN, a flow-matching framework using dynamic Betti curves as conditioning signals and a persistence landscape loss for homology consistency. It scales to multivariate data, includes a natural-language interface to set Betti targets, supports cross-domain meta-learning and few-shot generation, and provides certified adversarial robustness.
  On financial, epidemiological, and multi-modal benchmarks, PHINN outperforms statistical and diffusion baselines in topological fidelity (beta-RMSE down 41-63%, transition accuracy up 84%) and matches jump-diffusion models in tail coverage while exceeding them in shape fidelity. All results have 95% confidence intervals.

</details>


### [148] [Understanding Diversity Collapse in RLVR via the Lens of Overtraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15455)
*Suqin Yuan,Jinkun Chen,Jiyang Zheng,Muyang Li,Lei Feng,Dadong Wang,Tao Xiang,Tongliang Liu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）中的多样性坍塌问题，将其归因于"过训练"：当问题对参考指标的贡献饱和后，进一步更新只会集中概率质量而不会扩展推理边界。基于此发现，提出贝叶斯边界门控（BBG），通过估计每个问题对推理边界的边际贡献来优化训练，在多个推理基准上提升不同k值下的平均Pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能提升大模型推理能力，但存在多样性坍塌（Pass@1提升而高k的Pass@k下降）问题，现有研究缺乏对这一现象的形式化理解与系统性干预手段。

Method: 首先从过训练视角形式化多样性坍塌，指出少量rollout下单个成功即使问题进入饱和状态；进而提出贝叶斯边界门控（BBG），通过估计每个问题对推理边界的边际贡献来重定向优化，避免过训练。

Result: 仅对零成功率问题更新可提升Pass@256；标准RLVR训练中观察到部分初不可解问题变得可解；BBG在多个推理基准上提升平均Pass@k。

Conclusion: RLVR中高k Pass@k下降并不意味着未产生新的推理增益；通过正确识别并跳过已饱和问题的训练，可以同时保持低k和高k性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a key approach for enhancing the reasoning abilities of large language models. However, RLVR often suffers from \emph{diversity collapse}: Pass@$1$ improves while high-$k$ Pass@$k$ degrades, which is viewed as a narrowing of the model's reasoning boundary. We formalize this diversity collapse through the lens of \emph{overtraining}: once a problem's contribution to the reference metric has effectively saturated, further updates no longer expand what the model can solve but still concentrate probability mass on the trajectories favored by on-policy sampling. Under a standard setup with few rollouts per problem, even a single observed success places a problem in a nearly saturated regime for high-$k$ Pass@$k$, so most updates in standard RLVR are overtraining from the boundary perspective. This perspective also suggests a reading of whether RLVR can expand the model's reasoning abilities beyond the base model: since RLVR is structurally biased against high-$k$ Pass@$k$, its aggregate decline does not by itself mean that no new reasoning gains occurred. Interventionally, restricting updates to problems with zero observed success lifts Pass@$256$ above the base model on difficult benchmarks; observationally, a non-trivial fraction of initially unsolvable problems become solvable during standard RLVR training. Building on these findings, we propose \emph{Bayesian Boundary Gating} (BBG), which redirects optimization away from overtraining by estimating each problem's marginal contribution to the reasoning boundary. Across multiple reasoning benchmarks, BBG improves average Pass@$k$ across a wide range of $k$.

</details>


### [149] [Bayesian 3D Steerable CNNs: Enabling Equivariance and Uncertainty Quantification Simultaneously](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15479)
*Abhishek Keripale,Ponkrshnan Thiagarajan,Susanta Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯可转向CNN，在保持SE(3)-等变性的同时实现不确定性量化，通过变分推断和Bayes-by-Backpropagation优化，在分类精度和校准误差上表现优异，尤其在有分布偏移时提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可转向CNN保证了SE(3)-等变性，但其确定性本质无法进行不确定性量化，限制了需要置信度估计的应用场景。

Method: 在可转向CNN的基系数上引入后验分布，实现随机卷积核同时保持精确等变性；采用变分推断推导损失函数，通过Bayes-by-Backpropagation优化；将预测不确定性分解为认知不确定性和偶然不确定性。

Result: 竞争性的分类精度，期望校准误差0.0263；在加性高斯噪声引起的分布偏移下，比确定性模型提升高达6.17%；利用不确定性估计在84%测试数据上提升约4%精度；认知不确定性与预测误差之间存在统计显著的负相关。

Conclusion: 该框架将贝叶斯不确定性量化与等变CNN的归纳偏置统一起来，学习到的后验方差具有语义意义。

Abstract: Steerable convolutional neural networks (Steerable-CNNs) guarantee SE(3)-equivariance by parameterizing kernels as linear combinations of steerable basis functions, but their deterministic nature precludes uncertainty quantification - limiting their use in settings where confidence estimates are essential. We propose a Bayesian Steerable-CNN that places posterior distributions over the basis coefficients, yielding stochastic kernels while preserving equivariance exactly. The loss function of the model is obtained via variational inference and minimized by Bayes-by-Backpropagation. The framework admits a decomposition of predictive uncertainty into epistemic and aleatoric components. Empirically, the model attains competitive classification accuracy alongside an expected calibration error of 0.0263 and outperforms its deterministic counterpart by up to 6.17% under distributional shift induced by additive Gaussian noise. Furthermore, we leverage the model's uncertainty estimates to enhance its performance significantly, achieving a notable gain - approximately 4% higher accuracy across 84% of the test dataset. A statistically significant negative correlation between epistemic uncertainty and prediction error confirms that the learned posterior variance is semantically meaningful. The framework unifies Bayesian uncertainty quantification with the inductive bias of equivariant CNNs.

</details>


### [150] [Model Stealing Through the Lens of Model Multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15493)
*Eliott Baltz,Satoshi Hara,Ulrich Aïvodji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了模型窃取攻击中高保真代理模型等同于原始服务模型的传统假设。通过计算Rashomon Set（近乎同等精度模型集合）并评估其多样性，发现代理模型虽与目标模型保真度相似，但在公平性等关键性能指标上存在显著差异，质疑了二者在实际部署中的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为高保真代理模型能为攻击者提供与原始服务提供商相当的经济优势，本文旨在验证这一假设是否成立，即高保真度是否真的意味着代理模型与目标模型在部署相关属性上等价。

Method: 计算代理模型的Rashomon Set（近乎同等精度模型集合），使用多重性指标（歧义性、不一致性、Rashomon容量）和群体公平性指标评估其多样性，在表格、医学影像和NLP任务上基于真实数据集进行实验。

Result: 尽管代理模型与目标模型表现出相似的保真度，但在其他关键性能指标（如公平性）上存在显著差异，表明高保真代理模型在实际部署中并不等同于目标模型。

Conclusion: 高保真代理模型与目标模型在部署场景中并不等价，质疑了模型窃取攻击的传统假设，为模型知识产权保护提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Model stealing attacks, where adversaries create high-fidelity surrogate models, are a significant threat to the intellectual property of machine learning services. Conventional wisdom suggests these surrogates could provide adversaries with economic leverage comparable to the original service providers. This paper challenges this assumption by evaluating model stealing attacks beyond mere fidelity to the target model. Because query-based extraction provides only partial supervision of the target's input-output behavior, the surrogate is not uniquely identified: many near-optimal surrogates can achieve comparable fidelity while differing in deployment-relevant properties. Instead of performing a classic learning-based model stealing attack, we compute the Rashomon Set (i.e., the set of almost-equally-accurate models) of surrogate models, and evaluate its diversity using multiplicity metrics (ambiguity, discrepancy, and Rashomon Capacity) and group fairness metrics. Across tabular, medical imaging, and NLP tasks, our experiments on real-world datasets reveal that despite exhibiting similar fidelity to the target model, surrogate models can display significant variances in other critical performance metrics. These findings cast doubt on the presumed equivalence between high-fidelity surrogates and the target model in practical deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [151] [Towards Data-Efficient Cross-Device Generalization of Grad-Shafranov Equilibria via Transfer Learning Neural Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15512)
*Jay Phil Yoo,William Howes,Yashika Ghai,Kazuma Kobayashi,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将磁流体平衡重建重新表述为跨装置算子学习问题，开发了领域特定的神经算子框架，将几何和剖面参数直接映射到极向磁通场。在Solov'ev解析族上测试了五种神经算子架构和四种迁移学习策略，发现多几何预训练可实现数据高效的跨装置迁移，其中小波神经算子在跨几何性能上最优（100个目标平衡样本时相对L2误差低于4%，全微调低于2%），且四种架构实现毫秒级推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Grad-Shafranov平衡计算严重依赖特定装置且需要迭代求解，难以用于延迟受限的实时控制场景；同时已有的神经网络方法虽然能加速单个平衡预测，但无法在变化的等离子体边界或托卡马克几何之间提供可复用的模型。

Method: 采用神经算子框架，将平衡重建建模为从几何与剖面参数到极向磁通场的算子学习问题。以解析可解的Solov'ev族为受控测试平台，生成8种几何不同的托卡马克构型，测试5种神经算子架构（含小波神经算子），并在4种迁移学习策略下进行实验。

Result: 单几何预训练跨装置迁移性能差，而多几何预训练能实现数据高效的迁移。小波神经算子跨几何性能最强，100个标记目标平衡样本时相对L2误差低于4%，全微调低于2%。预测磁场满足无散约束至数值精度，四种架构推理时间达到毫秒甚至亚毫秒级。

Conclusion: 神经算子预训练是实现跨托卡马克装置可复用、实时平衡推理的有效途径。

Abstract: Real-time reconstruction of magnetohydrodynamic equilibria is essential for plasma shaping, stability assessment and feedback control in magnetic confinement fusion. However, Grad-Shafranov equilibrium calculations remain largely device-specific and iterative, limiting their use in latency-constrained control settings. Existing neural approaches can accelerate individual equilibrium predictions, but they do not generally provide reusable models across changing plasma boundaries or tokamak geometries. Here we show that equilibrium reconstruction can be recast as a cross-device operator learning problem. We develop a domain-specific neural operator framework that maps geometry and profile parameters directly to the poloidal flux field, replacing repeated solve-on-demand computation with amortized operator inference. Using the analytically tractable Solov'ev family as a controlled Grad-Shafranov testbed, we generate equilibria across eight geometrically distinct tokamak-like configurations and benchmark five neural operator architectures under four transfer-learning strategies. Single-geometry pretraining gives poor transfer to unseen devices, whereas multi-geometry pretraining enables data-efficient adaptation. The Wavelet Neural Operator gives the strongest cross-geometry performance, reaching mean relative L2 errors below 4% with 100 labelled target equilibria and below 2% with full fine-tuning. The predicted magnetic fields satisfy the divergence-free constraint to numerical precision, and four architectures achieve millisecond or sub-millisecond inference. These results identify neural operator pretraining as a route towards reusable, real-time equilibrium inference across fusion device configurations.

</details>


### [152] [Greedy Coordinate Diffusion: Effective and Semantically Coherent Adversarial Attacks via Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15531)
*Bohdan Turbal,Blossom Metevier,Max Springer,Aleksandra Korolova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个局部几何框架来分析对齐语言模型在微调中的脆弱性，证明了曲率引起的二阶加速会导致对齐退化，并形式化了对齐不稳定条件（AIC），实验表明Fisher信息矩阵可作为安全退化程度的代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有的机制方法揭示了对齐在模型权重中的位置，但缺乏一个通用的形式化框架来推导关于微调何时会退化对齐的保证，这使得该领域缺乏预测或防止对齐崩溃的原则性工具。

Method: 通过参数空间轨迹的几何分析建立局部几何框架；证明一阶正交更新的安全性是错觉，曲率诱导的二阶加速会导致对齐敏感区域的二阶漂移；形式化对齐不稳定条件（AIC）作为三个几何性质；证明梯度流轨迹上对齐退化的四次方爆发。

Result: 证明了静态一阶保护在梯度下降下失效的充分条件；对齐退化以四次方速度爆发，其程度由对齐对特定参数的敏感性和任务与这些参数的耦合强度决定；实验验证了Fisher信息矩阵可作为安全退化程度的有效代理。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个形式化框架来理解微调中对齐退化的几何根源，为预测和防止对齐崩溃提供了原则性工具，并证明了曲率效应是对齐脆弱性的核心机制。

Abstract: Fine-tuning aligned language models on benign tasks (e.g. math tutoring) systematically breaks safety guardrails, even when training data contains no harmful content. While mechanistic approaches have shed light on where alignment resides in model weights, they do not by provide a general formal framework for deriving guarantees about when fine-tuning degrades it -- leaving the field without principled tools for predicting or preventing alignment collapse. We develop a local geometric framework through geometric analysis of parameter-space trajectories and apply it to understand the fragility of alignment in fine-tuning. While first-order analysis suggests orthogonal updates are safe, we prove this is illusory: the curvature of the fine-tuning loss induces second-order acceleration that can induce second-order drift into alignment-sensitive regions. We formalize a construct of our framework as the Alignment Instability Condition (AIC), three geometric properties that, when present, are sufficient to guarantee degradation. Our main result proves quartic onset of alignment degradation along gradient-flow trajectories, determined by how sharply alignment depends on specific parameters and how strongly tasks couple to these parameters. These findings yield formal sufficient conditions under which static first-order protection can fail under gradient descent. We further empirically validate the framework's foundations, showing that the Fisher Information Matrix provides a proxy for the degree of safety degradation across diverse fine-tuning.

</details>


### [153] [A Bifurcation Theory Framework for Gradient Descent on the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15551)
*Eric Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现代深度学习中的Edge of Stability (EoS)现象，提出了一套适用于过参数化神经网络的分岔理论分析框架。通过将训练动力学分解为法向和切向分量，证明了稳定的EoS训练源于法向方向的flip分岔，由第一Lyapunov系数的符号控制，而切向动力学则向锐度减小的区域漂移。在温和的谱与几何假设下，证明了在EoS阈值处训练收敛到极小值流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EoS现象在现代深度学习中普遍存在，但以往严格分析大多局限于标量或低维损失函数且依赖特定结构形式，缺乏适用于现实场景（如过参数化神经网络）的理论框架。

Method: 将训练动力学分解为法向和切向分量，建立分岔理论框架。法向方向分析flip分岔及第一Lyapunov系数，切向方向分析锐度递减的漂移行为。

Result: 证明在EoS阈值处训练收敛到极小值流形；统一并推广了先前结果（如Gan (2026)的产品稳定性条件是其框架的一个实例）。

Conclusion: 该分岔理论框架为理解过参数化神经网络中的EoS现象提供了统一的理论基础，弥合了现有理论与实际应用之间的差距。

Abstract: The Edge of Stability (EoS) phenomenon, where gradient descent operates with sharpness exceeding the classical convergence threshold yet the loss decreases over long timescales, is ubiquitous in modern deep learning but remains poorly understood in realistic settings. Prior rigorous analyses have been largely confined to scalar or low-dimensional losses with specific structural forms. In this work, we develop a bifurcation theory framework for gradient descent on the edge of stability that applies directly to overparameterized neural networks. By decomposing the training dynamics into components normal and tangent to the manifold of minimizers, we show that stable EoS training arises from a flip bifurcation in the normal direction, governed by the sign of the first Lyapunov coefficient, while the tangent dynamics drift toward regions of decreasing sharpness. Under mild spectral and geometric assumptions on the loss landscape, we prove convergence to the minimizing manifold when training at the EoS threshold. As a corollary, we recover and unify prior results: we show that the product-stability condition of Gan (2026) is an instance of our framework.

</details>


### [154] [Distilling Drifting Transformers with Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15553)
*Jiawei Zhang,Mengfei Xia,Gen Li,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Drift-RAE方法，将漂移模型范式应用于表征自编码器潜在空间，通过理论对齐改进训练稳定性，在ImageNet 256上仅用10K蒸馏步数即达到1.77 FID，超越了现有RAE蒸馏方法且无需辅助MAE特征提取器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAE通过DINO特征提升了潜在空间的语义聚类性，但其各向异性和大曲率导致基于轨迹的蒸馏不稳定；现需探索RAE潜在空间与蒸馏的兼容性。

Method: 定量研究不同自编码器的曲率与各向同性统计量，理论上揭示漂移模型在重构型VAE的散乱空间易失败；提出Drift-RAE，将漂移范式直接应用于RAE潜在空间，并通过理论对齐漂移场与其他框架来提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在ImageNet 256上以10K蒸馏步数达到1.77 FID，超越现有RAE蒸馏方法，与原始漂移模型性能相当且无需辅助MAE特征提取器。

Conclusion: RAE潜在空间与基于漂移模型的蒸馏范式兼容，Drift-RAE有效解决了RAE空间的各向异性问题，实现了高效的扩散/流模型蒸馏。

Abstract: Representation Autoencoders (RAEs) have improved diffusion and flow models by semantically richer latent space owing to the strongly label-wise clustered DINO features in the pretrained encoders. Yet in the distillation stage, the severe anisotropy and large curvatures caused by the rich semantic representations would hinder the convergence and performance, making the trajectory-based distillation unstable. In this work, we argue that the RAE latent space is compatible with distillation via the newly proposed Drifting Models. We first quantitatively study the curvatures and isotropy statistics across different autoencoders, and theoretically reveal that Drifting Model itself is highly likely to fail on extremely scattered spaces like reconstruction-based VAEs. These motivate us to apply the drifting paradigm directly to representation autoencoders. Our proposed method, Drift-RAE, distills pretrained flow models in RAE latent spaces using Drifting, together with insightful modifications that improve training stability by thereotically aligning drifting fields with other frameworks. Regarding the experimental evidences, we achieve 1.77 FID on ImageNet 256 dataset using only 10k distillation steps, surpassing state-of-the-art RAE distillation methods and appearing comparative with the original Drifting Model without requiring an auxiliary MAE feature extractor. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [155] [A Decision-Theoretic View of Test-Time Training: When, How Far, and Which Directions to Adapt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15569)
*Tomoya Wakayama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从决策理论视角将测试时训练（TTT）解释为核机制下的隐式贝叶斯推理，揭示了其不稳定性源于超参数（更新步长、子空间）与提示信噪比及查询相关特征方向的失配，并提出了基于PAC-Bayes的步长选择、贝叶斯最优子空间评分等理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时训练（TTT）虽能提升预训练模型在分布偏移下的准确性，但其性能常因不稳定且对超参数（如更新步长、子空间）敏感而受限，缺乏理论解释。

Method: 将TTT建模为高斯过程核机制下的隐式贝叶斯推理，通过谱匹配分析更新步长与信号噪声比的关系、证明提示证据选择步长的PAC-Bayes泛化界、以及推导线性-高斯校正模型下的贝叶斯最优更新子空间评分规则。

Result: (1) 揭示了固定步长和子空间在分布偏移下的失效原因；(2) 证明了基于提示证据选择步长具有PAC-Bayes过拟合保证；(3) 刻画了贝叶斯最优更新子空间，为选择Transformer块和注意力头提供评分规则。

Conclusion: 理论解释了TTT的经验不稳定性，为何时、更新多远、沿什么方向适应提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) adapts a pretrained model to each prompt via parameter updates, improving accuracy under pretraining-to-test distribution shifts. Yet, its performance often suffers from instability and sensitivity to hyperparameters such as update steps and subspace. We explain this behavior through a decision-theoretic lens, treating TTT as implicit Bayesian inference in the kernel regime. Under a Gaussian process benchmark, we show that TTT reduces prediction error when updates are spectrally matched to the prompt's signal-to-noise ratio and aligned with query-relevant eigen-directions. This perspective underpins the following results: (1) we show when fixed update steps and subspaces fail under distribution shifts, motivating adaptive strategies; (2) we prove that selecting update steps via prompt evidence admits a PAC-Bayes guarantee against overfitting; and (3) we characterize the Bayes-optimal update subspace under a linear-Gaussian correction model, yielding a scoring rule for selecting Transformer blocks and heads. Our theory helps explain the empirical instability of TTT, taking a step toward principled guidance for when, how far, and which directions to adapt.

</details>


### [156] [Localizing Credit at the Divergence: Path-Conditioned Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15576)
*Yu Li,Shu Hong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hindsight Self-Distillation (HSD)方法，通过将教师模型条件设置为当前训练组中成功的同伴轨迹（而非仅真实答案），为失败轨迹提供密集的逐token信用分配信号。HSD在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-32B的数学与代码基准上 outperforms GRPO变体及自蒸馏基线，尤其在简洁答案任务（如AIME）上提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于可验证奖励的强化学习中，长推理轨迹存在token级信用分配不明确的问题。现有方法使用真实答案作为教师条件，导致在简洁答案任务中中间推理位置缺乏有效的路径级指导信号。

Method: HSD将教师模型条件设置为当前训练批次中成功的同伴轨迹（即成功条件策略的精确样本），通过比较失败轨迹与成功同伴在分歧位置上的差异，提供密集的逐token信用分配信号，无需额外采样。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-32B的数学与代码基准测试中，HSD相比GRPO变体及现有自蒸馏基线取得最优结果，尤其在AIME等简洁答案任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: HSD通过利用成功同伴轨迹作为条件，有效解决了简洁答案任务中中间推理位置缺乏信用分配信号的问题，在多种模型规模和任务上均取得最佳效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards assigns a single scalar to each rollout, leaving token-level credit assignment underspecified in long reasoning traces. On-policy self-distillation addresses this by letting the same model act as a teacher conditioned on privileged information, producing a dense per-token signal. But the common choice of a ground-truth answer is only an endpoint cue: on terse-answer tasks, the teacher falls silent at the intermediate positions where path-level guidance matters most. We propose Hindsight Self-Distillation (HSD), which conditions the teacher on a successful peer rollout drawn from the current training group. Such a peer is an exact sample from the success-conditioned policy, requiring no additional sampled rollouts. By providing a full successful continuation rather than only the final answer, the resulting credit signal concentrates at the divergence position between a failed rollout and a successful peer. Across Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B on math and code benchmarks, HSD obtains the best result against GRPO variants and on-policy distillation baselines, with the largest gains on terse-answer tasks such as AIME.

</details>


### [157] [Is Code Better Than Language for Algorithmic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15589)
*Terry Tong,Yu Feng,Surbhi Goel,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过分离中间表示与执行机制，发现工具增强型语言模型的优势来源于可靠的外部执行，而非中间表示变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究难以区分工具增强型语言模型中，自然语言推理与代码执行管道的性能差异究竟是源于中间表示的变化还是执行机制的变化。

Method: 设计了中间干预实验：让模型将推理表达为可执行代码，再由语言模型在上下文中模拟该代码产生答案。在40个可验证算法基准任务上，比较确定性代码执行、自然语言推理和中间干预三种管道的性能。同时构建统计决策理论模型刻画执行对端到端风险的支配机制，并利用代理模型从代码表示重建自然语言推理轨迹进行验证。

Result: 确定性代码执行比自然语言推理高出31.6个百分点；中间干预与自然语言推理无显著差异（+0.15pp）；重建干预恢复的性能与原始自然语言推理管道相当。

Conclusion: 在评估设定下，工具使用优势主要来自可靠的外部执行机制，而非中间表示的变化。

Abstract: For tool-augmented language models, comparing natural-language reasoning with code-execution pipelines is difficult because the comparison changes both the intermediate representation and the execution mechanism. We separate these factors with an intermediate intervention: the model expresses its reasoning as executable code, and the language model simulates that code in context to produce an answer. On a 40-task verifiable algorithmic benchmark, deterministic code execution outperforms natural-language reasoning by +31.6pp. We observe that the intermediate intervention is not meaningfully different from natural-language reasoning (+0.15pp). These results suggest that, in our evaluated setting, changing the intermediate representation alone does not explain the tool-use advantage, providing evidence for the performance gains requiring reliable external execution. We formalize this intuition with a simple statistical decision-theoretic model that characterizes when execution dominates end-to-end risk in our disentangled trace-generation/execution regime. We validate our theory using a reconstruction intervention that leverages a proxy language model to infer natural-language reasoning traces from code representations, recovering performance comparable to the original natural-language reasoning pipeline. All experiments are at https://github.com/TerryTong-Git/ToolProj.

</details>


### [158] [MoECa: Aligning Feature Reuse with Expert Decomposition in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15615)
*Maoliang Li,Haojing Chen,Jiayu Chen,Zihao Zheng,Xinhao Sun,Hailong Zou,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出MoECa，一种针对DiT-MoE模型的细粒度缓存框架，通过专家分支级别的特征复用实现了2.83倍推理加速且质量损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓存方法主要在token层级操作，但在DiT-MoE中每个token更新被分解为多个路由专家分支，token级缓存变得次优。作者发现跨时间步冗余在专家分支级别比token级别更明显，因此需要更细粒度的缓存策略。

Method: MoECa框架包含三个核心设计：1）分支级特征复用（跨时间步重用专家分支的计算结果）；2）专家感知的自适应控制（根据专家特性动态调整缓存策略）；3）同步缓存更新（在MoE和注意力路径间保持中间状态的稳定性）。

Result: 在多个DiT-MoE模型上的实验表明，MoECa相比现有缓存方法取得了更优的速度-质量权衡，实现最高2.83倍推理加速，同时仅带来极小的质量下降。

Conclusion: MoECa证明了在DiT-MoE模型中进行专家分支级别的细粒度缓存是有效的，为稀疏激活的扩散模型推理加速提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers with Mixture-of-Experts (DiT-MoE) improve model capacity under sparse activation, but diffusion inference is still bottlenecked by redundant computation across timesteps. Existing caching methods mainly operate at the token level, which becomes suboptimal in DiT-MoE because each token update is internally decomposed into multiple routed expert branches. Our analysis shows that cross-timestep redundancy in DiT-MoE is better characterized at the expert-branch level than at the whole-token level. Based on this observation, we propose MoECa, a fine-grained caching framework that performs branch-level feature reuse across timesteps. MoECa further introduces expert-aware adaptive control and synchronized cache updates across MoE and attention paths to maintain stable intermediate states. Experiments on multiple DiT-MoE models show that MoECa consistently achieves a better speed-quality trade-off than prior caching methods, with up to 2.83$\times$ inference speedup and minimal quality degradation.

</details>


### [159] [Re-feeding Is Not Replaying: Measuring Replay Noise in Counterfactual Token-Credit Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15621)
*Nils Matteson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了逐token反事实信用估计中“重新馈送”（re-feed）方法相对于精确恢复解码状态（exact resume）的偏差。在六个配置和三个模型上，re-feed在低裕度决策token上改变了信用估计结果，变化率比副本噪声基线高出14-28个百分点。虽然平均量大致安全，但基于阈值的关键token选择受到显著影响（Jaccard系数0.34-0.90 vs 副本上限0.63-0.96）。建议反事实信用研究应恢复解码器状态或使用批不变内核，并报告副本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐token反事实信用估计是理解语言模型决策过程的重要方法，但现有方法通过重新馈送前缀提示来模拟模型状态，这种近似是否准确尚未被系统量化。本文旨在测量这种近似假设在标准推理引擎上的实际代价。

Method: 采用三通道实验设计：从验证过的解码时KV状态恢复的精确恢复通道（exact resume）、完全相同的副本通道（replica noise floor）和重新馈送通道（re-feed）。在六个配置、三个模型（含GRPO训练检查点）上进行实验，测量低裕度决策token上的信用估计变化率，并通过vLLM的批不变内核进行因果验证。

Result: 重新馈送在低裕度决策token上改变了信用估计结果，变化率比副本噪声基线高出14-28个百分点（在治疗无关条件下为7-21pp，问题聚类t=2.9-6.4）。大多数变化是量化估计器的零边界跨越而非极性反转。基于阈值的关键token选择下，Jaccard系数为0.34-0.90，远低于副本上限0.63-0.96。在vLLM批不变内核下，三个通道在所有测量维度上完全一致。副本通道自身在9-23%的估计上存在分歧。

Conclusion: 反事实信用研究应恢复解码器状态或使用批不变内核，并必须报告副本基线以量化测量噪声。单样本信用测量在任意重放方式下都不可靠，平均量虽大致安全但选择敏感。

Abstract: Per-token counterfactual credit estimation asks which token in a language-model rollout caused the final answer to be right or wrong: cut the transcript at a pivot, substitute an alternative token, replay continuations, and compare outcomes. Published methods re-feed the transcript prefix as a fresh prompt, assuming this reproduces the state the model passed through during generation. We measure what that assumption costs on a stock inference engine, with a three-pass design: continuations resumed from the verified decode-time KV state, an identical second exact pass (a replica noise floor), and a re-feed pass. Across six configurations and three models (including a GRPO-trained checkpoint), at low-margin decision tokens, re-feeding changes the credit estimate at rates 14-28 percentage points above the replica floor (7-21pp under a treatment-independent conditioning; problem-clustered t = 2.9-6.4). Most changes are zero-boundary crossings of the quantized estimator rather than polarity reversals, and the perturbation is consistent with mean-zero, so averaged quantities are largely safe; but selection is not: a critical-token set chosen by thresholding $|\hat{A}_t|$ under re-feed overlaps the exact-resume selection at Jaccard 0.34-0.90, versus a 0.63-0.96 replica ceiling. A causal confirmation closes the loop: under vLLM's batch-invariant kernels all three passes are identical on every measured channel, with both disagreement rates exactly zero. Replica passes themselves disagree on 9-23% of eligible estimates: single-sample credit measurements at decision tokens are unreliable under any replay. Settings were fixed in advance; exact-pass cache hits in the second campaign are instrumented (100% hit rate, 3,434 pivots); total compute was under 10 USD. We recommend that counterfactual credit studies resume decoder state or use batch-invariant kernels, and report a replica floor.

</details>


### [160] [Surprise-Guided MergeSort: Budget-Efficient Human-in-the-Loop Ranking via Adaptive Comparison Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15623)
*Yujin Park,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SGS框架，利用VLM作为"问题优先级排序器"识别需要人类判断的关键比较，在排序任务中通过跳过非信息性比较来减少人工标注成本，在六个基准上取得Kendall's τ提升+6到+12。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对比较是主观排序的金标准，但穷举标注需要O(n²)量级的人工比较。即使排序方法将其降至O(n log n)，每次比较仍需高昂人力。目标是在保持排序质量的同时进一步减少人工标注量。

Method: 提出Surprise-Guided MergeSort (SGS)框架，包含三个组件：(1) 自底向上归并排序调度器，利用传递性；(2) 复合惊奇评分器，结合位置偏差消除的VLM置信度、Elo差距和投票熵来量化比较模糊性；(3) 自适应预算分配器，将高惊奇度的比较路由给人类，低惊奇度的通过传递性推理自动完成。

Result: 在六个基准（STS-B、BIOSSES、SICKR-STS、KonIQ-10k、TID2013、LIVE Challenge）上验证。SGS每轮有效识别并跳过最多535个非信息性比较，在相同预算下Kendall's τ×100比Active Elo提升+6到+12。

Conclusion: 结合VLM引导的惊奇度指标与算法排序，能在不同领域提供一致且良好的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Pairwise comparison is the gold standard for subjective ranking tasks; however, exhaustive annotation requires a massive number of human comparisons ($O(n^2)$). While sorting-based methods have reduced this burden to $O(n\log n)$, they still require expensive human judgment for every single comparison. To further improve annotation efficiency, we propose leveraging a Vision-Language Model (VLM) not as an annotator replacement, but as a \emph{question prioritizer} to identify which comparisons genuinely require human judgment. The proposed \textbf{Surprise-Guided MergeSort (SGS)} framework achieves this through three integrated components: (1) a bottom-up MergeSort scheduler that structures comparisons and exploits transitivity, (2) a composite Surprise Scorer -- combining position-bias-cancelled VLM confidence, Elo gap, and vote entropy -- to quantify comparison ambiguity, and (3) an adaptive budget allocator that routes high-surprise pairs to humans while automating low-surprise pairs via transitivity inference. Validation was conducted on six diverse benchmarks spanning text similarity (STS-B, BIOSSES, SICKR-STS) and image quality assessment (KonIQ-10k, TID2013, LIVE Challenge). SGS effectively identified and skipped up to 535 non-informative comparisons per session. Consequently, it achieved Kendall's $τ{\times}100$ improvements of $+6$ to $+12$ over Active Elo under the same total budget. These results demonstrate that combining VLM-guided surprise metrics with algorithmic sorting provides a generally consistent accuracy-efficiency trade-off across diverse domains.

</details>


### [161] [Conflict-Aware Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models with Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15625)
*Yijun Lu,Zihan Fang,Pengpeng Qiao,Zheng Lin,Jing Yang,Yuxin Zhang,Por Lip Yee,Zhe Chen,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中MoE模型因数据异构导致专家优化冲突的问题，提出FC-MoE框架，通过重要性加权、梯度共识投影和局部知识保留机制，实现稳定高效的联邦微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE在联邦学习数据异构环境下，客户端特定数据分布导致同索引专家在不同客户端上学习到不一致甚至冲突的特征-标签关联，聚合时产生破坏性干扰，影响模型性能。

Method: 提出FC-MoE框架：(1) 重要性感知加权方案，优先选择可靠的局部更新；(2) 梯度共识投影机制，抑制冲突更新；(3) 局部知识保留机制，通过重锚定领域特定残差保留客户端专长。

Result: 实验表明FC-MoE在非IID联邦环境中加速收敛，同时提升了全局和局部模型性能。

Conclusion: FC-MoE通过冲突感知机制有效解决了数据异构下MoE联邦微调中的专家优化冲突问题，实现了隐私保护下的高效协同优化。

Abstract: The continuous scaling of large language models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational costs, making Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) a scalable alternative for efficient fine-tuning via sparse activation. While federated learning (FL) emerges as the paradigm for privacy-preserving collaborative optimization, integrating MoE into FL under data heterogeneity may trigger conflicting expert optimizations. Client-specific data distributions force same-indexed experts to optimize under inconsistent or even conflicting feature-label correlations. This mismatch induces destructive interference during aggregation, thus destabilizing the optimization trajectory and degrading model performance. To address this issue, we propose FC-MoE, a federated conflict-aware framework for MoE fine-tuning. It employs an importance aware weighting scheme to prioritize reliable local updates and utilizes gradient consensus projection to suppress conflicting updates, ensuring a stable global optimization path. Moreover, a local knowledge retention mechanism further preserves specialized client expertise by re-anchoring domain-specific residuals. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FC-MoE accelerates convergence and enhances both global and local model performance in non-IID federated environments.

</details>


### [162] [Formalizing and Mitigating Structural Distortion in LLM Attention for Zero-Shot Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15633)
*Donald Loveland,Puja Trivedi,Ari Weinstein,Edward W Huang,Danai Koutra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了将大语言模型应用于文本属性图时，图线性化导致的旋转位置编码带宽依赖性注意力衰减问题，并提出轻量级推理时修正方法GaLA，通过在保持序列归纳偏好的同时偏置注意力到图相邻节点，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将LLMs应用于TAGs时存在图线性化带来的结构失真问题，但此前研究多归因于提示设计或模型规模，缺乏对底层机制的理解。本文旨在揭示失真背后的根本原因——旋转位置编码导致带宽依赖的注意力衰减，并证明该问题是可纠正的瓶颈而非不可逾越的障碍。

Method: 首先从理论上分析旋转位置嵌入如何将图线性化转化为带宽依赖的注意力衰减，即图中相邻节点在序列中被强制分开时注意力被抑制。基于此分析，提出Graph-aligned Language Attention (GaLA)，一种轻量级的推理时修改方法，通过对注意力进行偏置来增强图相邻节点间的关注，同时保留LLM原有的序列归纳偏好。

Result: 在多个TAG基准测试上，GaLA以可忽略的额外开销显著提升了LLM在图推理任务上的性能，验证了图线性化失真是一个可纠正的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 图线性化导致的注意力失真是LLM在图推理中的一个可纠正瓶颈，通过修正注意力对齐而非改进提示或扩大模型规模即可有效缓解。这为LLM在图结构化数据上的应用提供了新的研究视角和实用的改进方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for reasoning over Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs). However, applying LLMs to graphs requires linearizing their structure into sequences, introducing distortion rooted in the graph bandwidth problem. While this distortion has been shown to degrade performance, it is often attributed to prompt design or model scale, leaving the underlying mechanism unclear. In this work, we show \textit{how} rotary positional embeddings turn graph linearization into bandwidth-dependent attention decay, suppressing attention between graph-adjacent nodes that are forced far apart in the serialized sequence. This shifts the focus of LLM-based graph reasoning from prompt engineering and scaling toward correcting attention misalignment. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \textbf{G}raph-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{L}anguage \textbf{A}ttention (\textbf{GaLA}), a lightweight, inference-time modification for LLMs. GaLA biases attention toward graph-adjacent nodes while preserving the LLM's sequential inductive biases. Across TAG benchmarks, GaLA improves performance with negligible overhead, demonstrating that distortion is a correctable bottleneck in LLM-based graph reasoning.

</details>


### [163] [HAPI-EP: Towards Hybrid, Adaptive, and Predictive Digital Twins of Cardiac Electrophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15637)
*Sumeet Vadhavkar,Xiajun Jiang,Yubo Ye,Maryam Toloubidokhti,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HAPI是一个AI框架，通过构建物理集成的灰盒模型（机理骨干+神经残差建模）、利用前馈元学习器实现实时自适应（少样本在线数据适配）、以及条件生成模型赋予理论可辨识性，解决了心脏数字孪生在快速动态适配和预测能力方面的核心挑战。在心电生理学混合单域模型上的实验证明，其机理-神经混合与预测性适配对获得强预测和分布外能力的可辨识数字孪生至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏数字孪生（DT）在个性化医疗中具有巨大潜力，但其快速动态适配个体实时数据以及适配后的预测能力仍是核心挑战。现有方法中，机理模型与数据驱动模型各有优劣，而DT优化策略主要基于重建目标，导致模型不可辨识。

Method: 提出HAPI框架，包含三个关键组件：1) 物理集成的灰盒模型，用可解释的机理骨干加神经组件建模残差；2) 前馈元学习器实现混合模型参数（机理和神经参数）的摊销推理，支持少样本实时适配；3) 将适配过程构建为条件生成模型，赋予理论可辨识性。

Result: 在心电生理学中使用混合单域模型（机理反应动力学+神经图扩散）进行验证，在合成数据和真实数据上证明HAPI的机理-神经混合与预测性适配对于获得具有强预测能力和分布外泛化能力的可辨识数字孪生至关重要。

Conclusion: HAPI通过机理与数据驱动的混合建模、元学习驱动的快速自适应以及条件生成模型可辨识性理论三个层面的创新，有效解决了心脏数字孪生的动态适配与预测能力挑战。

Abstract: A digital twin (DT) of a patient-specific heart offers significant potential in personalized medicine. However, its rapid and dynamic adaptation to an individual's live data and its predictive capability after adaptation remains central challenges. We examine this challenge from its two building blocks: DT formulation where mechanistic and data-driven models show competing merits and limitations, and DT optimization strategies that are largely driven by a reconstruction objective leading to un-identifiable models. We address both bottlenecks via HAPI -- an AI framework for building hybrid, adaptive, and predictive DTs with three key enablers. First, HAPI constructs a physics-integrated gray-box model in which an interpretable mechanistic backbone is augmented by a neural component that models its residual to the observed data. Second, rather than attempting to pre-encode all possible variations in a static hybrid model, HAPI enables rapid on-the-fly adaptation of the hybrid model to few-shot live data, achieved by feedforward meta-learners realizing amortized inference of both mechanistic and neural parameters of the hybrid model trained with predictive objectives. Finally, we show that this adaptivity corresponds to the construction of a conditional generative model (i.e., the hybrid DT) that endows it with theoretical identifiability and thus strong performance in predictive scenarios. We demonstrate the proof-of-concept of HAPI in cardiac electrophysiology using a hybrid monodomain model with mechanistic reaction kinetics and neural graph diffusion. Across synthetic and real-data studies, we show that HAPI's mechanistic-neural hybridization and predictive adaptation are critical for obtaining identifiable DTs with strong predictive and out-of-distribution capabilities.

</details>


### [164] [Multi-Agent Framework for Audit Risk Assessment with Explicit Uncertainty and Evidence Conflict Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15640)
*Yuhan Wang,Manqing Wang,Yixuan Lu,Zhaoyue Peng,Shengda Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UMAR框架，通过三个专业智能体（MD&A文本、财务比率、CAM）分别生成带校准不确定性估计的独立风险评分，并利用Dempster-Shafer证据理论融合评分并量化智能体间冲突。在3200个公司年观测数据上，UMAR在AUROC、PR-AUC和校准误差方面均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计风险评估中整合异构证据源日益重要，但现有方法仅生成点预测，未能量化不同证据流之间的一致程度，导致审计师难以判断证据可靠性。

Method: 构建三个专业智能体（MD&A文本分析、财务比率分析、CAM分析），各自输出带校准不确定性估计的风险评分；采用基于Dempster-Shafer证据理论的不确定性聚合器融合评分，并显式测量智能体间的证据冲突。

Result: 在2019-2023年美国SEC 10-K报表的3200个公司年观测数据上，以财务重述为目标标签，UMAR取得AUROC 0.782、PR-AUC 0.341，优于逻辑回归、XGBoost、FinBERT及单/双智能体LLM基线；取得最低期望校准误差（ECE=0.052）；识别出与实际重述风险相关的证据冲突模式。

Conclusion: UMAR通过不确定性感知的多智能体协作与证据融合，不仅提升了审计风险评估的预测性能，还提供了可解释、可操作的冲突信号，有助于审计师做出更可靠的判断。

Abstract: Audit risk assessment increasingly benefits from combining heterogeneous evidence sources, yet existing approaches typically produce point predictions without quantifying how well different evidence streams agree. We propose UMAR (Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Agent Risk Assessment), a framework that employs three specialized agents: an MD&A Text Agent, a Financial Ratio Agent, and a CAM Agent, each producing independent risk scores with calibrated uncertainty estimates. An Uncertainty Aggregator based on Dempster-Shafer evidence theory fuses these scores while explicitly measuring inter-agent conflict. We evaluate UMAR on a U.S. dataset of 3,200 firm-year observations from SEC 10-K filings (2019-2023), with financial restatement as the target label. Experimental results show that UMAR achieves an AUROC of 0.782 and a PR-AUC of 0.341, outperforming logistic regression, XGBoost, FinBERT, and single-agent and dual-agent LLM baselines. UMAR attains the lowest expected calibration error (ECE = 0.052) among all methods and identifies evidence-conflict patterns that correlate with actual restatement risk, offering auditors potentially actionable and interpretable risk signals.

</details>


### [165] [CIWI-CKT: Chaos-Informed Wave Interference Feature Fusion and Cross-City Knowledge Transfer for Traffic Flow Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15642)
*Abdul Joseph Fofanah,Lian Wen,David Chen,Shaoyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIWI-CKT框架，融合混沌信息与波干涉特征，通过跨城市知识迁移实现数据稀缺场景下的精准交通流预测，在四个真实数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城市数据稀缺场景下，交通流预测因混沌动力学特性、复杂时空依赖及城市网络异质性而面临模型泛化困难；现有深度学习方法将交通视为纯确定性过程，缺乏建模波状干涉模式的能力，难以应对跨状态交通动态。

Method: 提出CIWI-CKT框架，包含三大创新：混沌信息驱动的波生成（提取混沌不变量并建模为自适应波分量）；元干涉处理（捕获支持集与查询集间的波交互，生成可预测性分数）；混沌感知元学习（在保留混沌特征的同时实现高效跨城市知识迁移）。同时建立包括混沌-波稳定性、波诱导降维和元学习泛化界在内的理论保证。

Result: 在四个真实世界交通数据集上的实验表明，CIWI-CKT显著优于现有最先进的时空图学习、迁移学习、提示学习和少样本方法，在提高预测精度的同时大幅减少所需训练数据。

Conclusion: CIWI-CKT通过将混沌理论与波干涉机制融入元学习框架，有效解决了跨城市少样本交通流预测中的模型泛化问题，理论与实验共同验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Accurate traffic flow prediction remains challenging in cross-city, data-scarce scenarios where limited historical data hinders model generalisation. The chaotic nature of traffic dynamics, complex spatio-temporal dependencies, and heterogeneous urban networks complicate few-shot learning across cities. Existing deep learning approaches either treat traffic as purely deterministic or lack mechanisms to model wave-like interference patterns essential for cross-regime traffic dynamics. To address these limitations, this paper proposes CIWI-CKT, a novel Chaos-Informed Wave Interference Feature Fusion framework with Cross-City Knowledge Transfer. Our framework introduces three core innovations: chaos-informed wave generation that extracts measurable chaos invariants and models traffic as adaptive wave components; meta-interference processing that captures wave interactions between support and query regimes while producing a predictability score for confidence estimation; and chaos-aware meta-learning that enables efficient cross-city knowledge transfer while preserving chaotic characteristics. We establish theoretical guarantees including chaos-to-wave stability, wave-induced dimension reduction, and meta-learning generalisation bounds. Extensive experiments on four real-world traffic datasets demonstrate that CIWI-CKT significantly outperforms state-of-the-art spatio-temporal graph learning, transfer learning, prompt-based, and few-shot methods, improving prediction accuracy while substantially reducing required training data.

</details>


### [166] [MosaicQuant: Inlier-Outlier Disaggregation for Unified 4-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15652)
*Yangjia Hu,Haodong Wang,Zicong Hong,Qianli Liu,Quanxin Shou,Jian Lin,Song Guo,Xiaowei Shen,Xiangjun Huang,Dian Wang,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MosaicQuant提出了一种统一的4比特LLM量化范式，通过“内点-离群点解耦”原则，将权重矩阵量化为密集4比特基分量+稀疏4比特残差分量，配合ZipperEngine将稀疏块计算融合到密集4比特GEMM内核中，在保持近FP16精度的同时实现最高1.24倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4比特量化难以同时忠实表示密集的常见值（内点）和罕见的大幅值（离群点），导致精度严重下降。混合精度方法虽保留离群点高精度，但破坏了低比特执行的统一性，引入精度转换和额外数据移动，削弱了实际加速效果。

Method: 提出MosaicQuant：将完整权重矩阵量化为密集4比特基分量（忠实捕捉内点，离群点被强制量化），再引入稀疏4比特残差分量补偿最关键误差权重块的量化误差。同时提出ZipperEngine，通过重叠流水线将稀疏块计算融合到密集4比特GEMM内核中，实现表示和执行的统一低比特推理流水线。

Result: 在LLaMA3和Qwen3上的实验表明，MosaicQuant在保持近FP16精度的同时，相比W16A16基线实现了最高1.24倍的加速。

Conclusion: 通过内点-离群点解耦和统一的低比特表示与执行，MosaicQuant在不牺牲精度的情况下有效实现了4比特LLM量化的实际加速。

Abstract: 4-bit quantization significantly reduces the memory footprint and accelerates the inference of large language models (LLMs). However, its limited bit-width representation struggles to faithfully capture both dense common values (\emph{inliers}) and rare large-magnitude values (\emph{outliers}), causing substantial accuracy degradation. Existing mixed-precision methods mitigate this by retaining outliers in high precision, but at the cost of breaking the uniformity of low-bit execution, introducing precision conversion and extra data movement that undermine practical speedup. We propose \textbf{MosaicQuant}, a unified 4-bit LLM quantization paradigm built on a novel principle of \emph{inlier--outlier disaggregation}. Rather than elevating outlier precision, MosaicQuant quantizes the full weight matrix into a dense 4-bit base component, where inliers are captured faithfully while outlier are inevitably quantized. A sparse 4-bit residual component is then introduced to compensate for these quantization errors, selectively targeting the most error-critical weight blocks where output distortion is shown to be concentrated. However, a unified representation alone is insufficient, as naïvely executing the sparse residual as a separate kernel still breaks the unified low-bit inference pipeline. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{ZipperEngine}, which fuses sparse block computation into the dense 4-bit GEMM kernel via an overlapped pipeline, unifying not only the representation but also the execution into a single coherent low-bit inference pipeline. Extensive experiments on LLaMA3 and Qwen3 demonstrate that MosaicQuant preserves near-FP16 accuracy while achieving up to $1.24\times$ speedup over the W16A16 baseline.

</details>


### [167] [Z-Plane Neural Networks: Bounded Geometric Activation Replaces ReLU and LayerNorm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15669)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Z-Plane神经网络，将隐藏状态映射到超球面上的2D相量束，使用Radial Bounding几何激活函数替代ReLU和LayerNorm，通过保持相位（方向）信息实现深度网络的稳定训练。100层MLP在MNIST上达到98.34%准确率且数值稳定，证明有界几何激活足以实现深度学习的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度神经网络依赖欧几里得标量激活（如ReLU）和全局归一化（如LayerNorm）来防止梯度不稳定，但这些机制固有地导致死神经元、丢弃关键方向信息并破坏特征表示的正交性。受生物轴突调频传输启发，作者希望设计一种能同时解决这些问题的替代方案。

Method: 提出Z-Plane神经网络，将隐藏状态映射到超球面上的2D相量束；引入Radial Bounding几何激活函数（x/max(1, ||x||₂)），限制能量幅度同时保留相位（方向）；从数学上证明该各向同性激活保持1-Lipschitz连续性，通过保留切向梯度防止梯度消失。

Result: 100层Z-Plane MLP（完全不含ReLU和LayerNorm）在MNIST数据集上成功收敛，达到98.34%准确率，且绝对数值稳定。

Conclusion: 有界几何激活单独足以实现稳定的深度学习，为摆脱传统标量激活和归一化机制提供了理论依据和实践验证。

Abstract: Modern deep neural networks rely on Euclidean scalar activations (e.g., ReLU) and global normalization techniques (e.g., LayerNorm) to prevent gradient instability in deep architectures. However, these mechanisms inherently cause dead neurons, discard critical directional information, and destroy the orthogonality of feature representations. Inspired by the frequency-modulation transmission of biological axons, we propose the Z-Plane Neural Network, which maps hidden states into 2D phasor bundles on a hypersphere. We introduce a novel geometric activation function, Radial Bounding($\mathbf{x} / \max(1, \|\mathbf{x}\|_2)$), which limits the energy magnitude while preserving the phase (direction). We demonstrate mathematically that this isotropic activation maintains 1-Lipschitz continuity and prevents gradient vanishing by preserving tangential gradients. Empirically, a 100-layer Z-Plane Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP)-entirely devoid of ReLU and LayerNorm-successfully converges on the MNIST dataset with 98.34% accuracy and absolute numerical stability, proving that bounded geometric activation alone is sufficient for stable deep learning.

</details>


### [168] [The Reservoir Attention Network: Cross-Pass State in Pretrained Transformers via Content-Addressable Reservoir Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15678)
*Emma Leonhart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了 Reservoir Attention Network (RAN)，通过在预训练 Transformer 的中间层注意力中注入一个固定、随机初始化的残差池（Reservoir）来跨前向传递携带状态。实验在 GPT-2 (124M, 355M) 和 Qwen2.5 (0.5B, 1.5B) 上进行，使用单个消费级 GPU，验证了未经训练的循环动态本身即可携带可用的跨传递状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否仅通过未经训练的循环动态（固定随机残差池）就能在 Transformer 的连续前向传递之间携带可用状态，从而为更高效的 always-alive agent 提供计算受限条件下的替代方案。

Method: 在预训练 Transformer 的中间层注意力中注入一个固定、随机初始化的残差池，使其在前向传递之间保持状态；残差池保持未训练状态，以隔离纯循环动态的贡献。

Result: 在 GPT-2 (124M, 355M) 和 Qwen2.5 (0.5B, 1.5B) 上，使用单个消费级 GPU 进行最小探针实验，结果表明未训练的循环动态即可携带可用的跨传递状态。

Conclusion: 固定随机残差池能够有效携带跨前向传递的状态，证明了未经训练的循环动态在 Transformer 架构中的可行性；训练循环被视为互补但更昂贵的方向，留给未来工作。

Abstract: A feasibility and dynamics study of the Reservoir Attention Network (RAN), an architecture that injects a fixed, randomly-initialized reservoir into the mid-layer attention of a pretrained transformer to carry state across forward passes. Experiments span GPT-2 (124M, 355M) to Qwen2.5 (0.5B, 1.5B) on a single consumer GPU. The tasks are minimal probes chosen to isolate individual mechanisms; the broader always-alive agent vision is treated throughout as compute-limited future work, not a claim of this paper. The reservoir is left untrained (fixed random) by design: this isolates whether untrained recurrent dynamics alone suffice to carry usable cross-pass state, leaving trained recurrence as a complementary, more expensive direction.

</details>


### [169] [ReQAT: Achieving Full-Precision Reasoning Accuracy with 4-bit Floating-Point Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15682)
*Janghwan Lee,Sihwa Lee,Jinseok Kim,Yongjik Kim,Jieun Lim,Jinwook Oh,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReQAT通过推理感知的FP4量化训练框架，在W4A4KV4全量化下不仅恢复了大推理模型的推理能力，还超越了BF16微调精度，并实现了最高3.9倍的吞吐加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRMs因长思维链和全精度推理的高成本及KV缓存开销而部署受限；现有FP4量化（W4A4KV4）导致严重的推理退化，且PTQ和QAT方法均无法有效恢复。

Method: 提出ReQAT框架，包含三个组件：Trace-Aligned QAT (TAQ) 通过复现相同推理轨迹聚焦低熵关键决策的更新；Selective Entropy Minimization (SEM) 增强低熵位置的置信度；Q-FIT 通过联合校准RoPE一致的KV缓存变换来稳定QAT初始化。

Result: ReQAT在相同训练预算下不仅恢复且超越BF16微调精度；在NVIDIA DGX Spark上实现3.9倍吞吐加速，在B200上实现3.1倍加速。

Conclusion: FP4量化退化的根源在于低熵token（数字、运算符等精确符号承诺）上的量化噪声引发采样误差级联放大；ReQAT通过推理感知的训练框架有效解决了该问题。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong problem-solving through long chain-of-thought, but their deployment is constrained by the high cost of full-precision inference and growing KV cache footprints. Microscaled FP4 formats enable efficient FP4 deployment; however, fully quantizing weights, activations, and KV caches (W4A4KV4) causes severe reasoning degradation that existing PTQ and QAT fail to recover. We identify that FP4 failures concentrate on low-entropy tokens--precise symbolic commitments such as digits and operators--where quantization noise inflates sampling errors that cascade through reasoning traces. Based on this insight, we propose ReQAT, a reasoning-centric FP4 training framework with three components: (i) Trace-Aligned QAT (TAQ), which revisits identical reasoning traces to focus updates on critical low-entropy decisions; (ii) Selective Entropy Minimization (SEM), which reinforces confidence at low-entropy positions; and (iii) Q-FIT, a quantization-friendly initialization that jointly calibrates RoPE-consistent KV cache transformations to stabilize QAT. Under the same training budget, ReQAT not only recovers but surpasses BF16 fine-tuning accuracy, while delivering up to 3.9x throughput speedup on NVIDIA DGX Spark and 3.1x on B200.

</details>


### [170] [Multi-Fidelity SINDy: Sparse Discovery of Nonlinear Dynamical Systems with Fidelity-Weighted Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15690)
*Filippo Zacchei,Ana Larrañaga,Attilio Frangi,Andrea Manzoni,Steven L. Brunton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异构噪声和变精度数据的非线性动力学系统辨识问题，本文在SINDy框架中融合集成SINDy与弱SINDy，通过广义最小二乘加权回归处理异方差噪声，在多基准系统（含ODE和PDE）及双摆多保真预测任务中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真与实验数据普遍存在噪声且保真度异构，测量不确定性因观测重复性、传感设备差异甚至单次实验内部而变化，现有方法难以从这种非均匀数据中可靠地发现非线性动力学系统。

Method: 将集成SINDy（Ensemble SINDy）与弱SINDy（Weak SINDy）相结合，通过广义最小二乘（Generalized Least Squares）推导出加权回归公式，对可变噪声水平进行统计加权，并给出加权策略的统计理论依据。

Result: 在常微分方程和偏微分方程基准系统上验证有效，双摆系统多保真度集成预测表明：重复的低成本、低质量测量有望匹配甚至超越仅用高保真数据的重建效果。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效缓解异方差噪声的负面影响，且多保真度集成策略在数据获取成本受限时具有实用优势。

Abstract: Data from simulations and experiments are rarely noise-free and often exhibit heterogeneous levels of fidelity. Measurement uncertainty may vary across repeated observations, sensing devices, or even within a single experiment. This work addresses the problem of discovering nonlinear dynamical systems from such inhomogeneous data. We extend the Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamical Systems (SINDy) framework to account for variable noise levels by combining Ensemble SINDy and Weak SINDy within a weighted regression formulation derived from generalized least squares. A statistical justification for the weighting strategy is also provided. The methodology is validated on several benchmark systems, including ordinary and partial differential equations. In addition, we show the benefit of multi-fidelity integration for forecasting the dynamics of a double pendulum system. The results confirm that the proposed approach mitigates the adverse effects of heteroscedastic noise and that repeated, low-cost, low-quality measurements can improve model recovery, in some cases matching or outperforming reconstructions obtained using only high-fidelity data.

</details>


### [171] [When Generator Replay Degrades: Projected Rehearsal Orchestration for Heterogeneous Federated Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15695)
*Thinh T. H. Nguyen,Khoa D. Doan,Binh T. Nguyen,Danh Le-Phuoc,Kok-Seng Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PRO和PRO-MAX框架，通过投影重放编排（projected rehearsal orchestration）解决异构流下联邦类增量学习（FCIL）中客户端标签子集不一致、任务阶段不同步及监督不平衡等问题，无需外部预训练和输入空间合成，在图像、文本、图基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FCIL方法依赖输入空间合成重放，在异构任务流下表现脆弱且跨模态迁移困难；客户端观察到不同标签子集、不同任务阶段、对相同语义概念提供不均等监督，导致旧知识难以有效保留。

Method: PRO：服务器维护紧凑的类级投影记忆（projected memories），客户端对当前样本与旧投影记忆进行平衡的伪多任务训练；PRO-MAX：进一步引入邻域加权记忆对齐（neighborhood-weighted memory alignment），保持服务器仅聚合模型更新和记忆统计的轻量原则。

Result: 在图像、文本、图三个模态基准上，PRO和PRO-MAX在异构流下显著提升保留率和最终效用，在同类FCIL中保持竞争力；即使基线方法获得更大重放预算，在监督不平衡和阶段错位下降解仍严重。

Conclusion: 重放数量本身无法解决重放质量失败问题；投影记忆与演化表征的更好对齐是提升FCIL异构流下性能的关键。弱任务诊断进一步表明重放失配越大，下游退化越严重。

Abstract: Federated class-incremental learning (FCIL) becomes substantially harder when clients observe different label subsets, progress through tasks at different stages, and provide uneven supervision for the same semantic concepts. Existing FCIL methods often preserve old knowledge through input-space synthesis, but they can be fragile under heterogeneous task streams and difficult to transfer across modalities. To alleviate such issues, we propose PRO, a framework that replaces synthetic input replay with projected rehearsal orchestration. To remove external pretraining, we evaluate all methods under the same warmup. After this, PRO maintains compact class-level projected memories on the server and allows clients perform balanced pseudo multi-task training over current examples and old projected memories. To handle stronger representation drift, we further introduce PRO-MAX, which augments PRO with neighborhood-weighted memory alignment while preserving the same server-light principle that the server only aggregates model updates and memory statistics. Across image, text, and graph benchmarks, PRO and PRO-MAX improve retention and final utility under heterogeneous streams while remaining competitive in homogeneous FCIL. Even when baselines are given expanded replay budgets, they degrade under supervision imbalance and stage misalignment, indicating that replay quantity alone does not resolve replay-quality failures. Additional weak-task diagnostics further show that larger replay mismatch is associated with larger downstream degradation, while our method keeps projected memories better aligned with the evolving representation.

</details>


### [172] [Robust Transformer-Based One-Step Stock Index Forecasting via Shifted Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15701)
*Tien Thanh Thach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的Transformer架构，结合余弦退火学习率调度和移位数据增强(SDA)技术，用于股票指数一步预测。实验表明，余弦退火加预热优于逆幂调度，SDA能显著降低预测误差和运行间方差，且组合方法在VN30和S&P 500上取得最佳效果，证明数据增强比增加模型复杂度更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在金融时间序列预测中面临噪声大、短记忆动态和分布漂移等挑战，直接应用效果不佳，需要针对金融数据特点进行改进。

Method: 提出改进的Transformer架构用于单步股票指数预测，结合余弦退火加预热的学习率调度策略，以及创新的Shifted Data Augmentation (SDA)数据增强技术。

Result: 1) 余弦退火加预热策略一致优于广义逆幂调度；2) SDA显著降低预测误差和运行间变异性，提升对超参数选择的鲁棒性；3) 余弦退火与SDA组合在VN30和S&P 500上取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 在基于Transformer的金融预测中，数据增强比增加模型复杂度发挥更重要的作用；该方法为噪声金融市场中的稳健股票指数预测提供了计算高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformers have shown remarkable success in sequence modeling, yet their direct application to financial time series remains challenging due to noisy signals, short-memory dynamics, and distributional shifts. This paper proposes a modified Transformer architecture for one-step stock index forecasting, combined with advanced learning-rate scheduling and a novel Shifted Data Augmentation (SDA) technique. We evaluate the proposed framework on two benchmark stock index datasets, VN30 and S&P 500. Experimental results demonstrate that cosine annealing with warmup consistently improves forecasting accuracy over the generalized inverse-power scheduler. Furthermore, SDA substantially reduces forecasting errors and run-to-run variability while improving robustness to hyperparameter selection. The combination of cosine annealing scheduling and SDA achieved the best performance on both datasets, indicating that data augmentation can play a more important role than increasing model complexity in Transformer-based financial forecasting. These findings provide a practical and computationally efficient approach for robust stock index forecasting in noisy financial environments.

</details>


### [173] [How to Score Experts for One-Shot MoE Expert Pruning: A Unified Formulation and Selection Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15716)
*Zongfang Liu,Jinghui Zhang,Zijian Ma,Guangyi Chen,Xin Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对MoE语言模型的一次性专家剪枝问题，提出了一个统一公式化框架，并据此推导出剪枝准则选择原则：任务无关剪枝应使用基于路由令牌平均的无门控激活准则，任务特定剪枝则可保留路由频率和门权重信息。基于此原则提出了两个新准则MAN和MSAN，在四个MoE模型和16个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一次性专家剪枝准则多为启发式，且没有单一准则普遍最优，因此需要建立原则性方法来选择适合不同部署目标的剪枝准则，这一问题此前未被充分探索。

Method: 提出了一个统一公式化框架，将一次性MoE专家剪枝组织为三个因素：路由频率、门控权重和激活强度。基于此框架推导出剪枝准则选择原则，并设计了两个新准则：Mean Activation Norm (MAN) 和 Mean Squared Activation Norm (MSAN)。

Result: 在四个代表性MoE模型和16个多样化基准测试中，MAN和MSAN在任务无关设置下表现一致优异，获得前两名的平均排名，相比最强基线平均性能提升高达8.8个点。

Conclusion: 该工作为一次性MoE专家剪枝提供了理论指导原则，并证明了基于路由令牌平均的无门控激活准则在任务无关剪枝中的有效性。
