TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑的谱表示方法，将持久拉普拉斯算子（Persistent Laplacian）蒸馏为三个数学不变量——贝蒂数、谱间隙和解析挠率，以解决高维度和“变长”问题。在MNIST、QM-3D和SKEMPI WT等基准数据集上的实验表明，该降维特征空间能够捕捉全谱的预测信号，有时甚至超越全谱，同时显著降低计算开销并避免高频噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久拉普拉斯算子虽能提供比持久同调更丰富的几何表示，但其完整的特征谱用于学习任务时面临高维度和不同过滤尺度下的“变长”问题，亟需一种紧凑且长度固定的表示方法。

Method: 将持久拉普拉斯算子蒸馏为三个数学上严格的不变量：贝蒂数（Betti numbers）、谱间隙（spectral gap）和解析挠率（analytic torsion），形成固定长度的紧凑谱表示。

Result: 在MNIST、QM-3D和SKEMPI WT数据集上，该降维特征空间能够捕捉全谱的预测信号，部分情况下表现更优，同时显著降低计算开销并避免高频特征值引入的噪声。

Conclusion: 这三个不变量为谱几何与拓扑学习之间提供了一个原则性的、固定长度的接口，表明这种紧凑表示是一种有效且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: While persistent Laplacians (PL) offer a richer geometric representation of data than persistent homology, utilizing their full eigenspectrum for learning tasks is often hampered by high dimensionality and the ``varying length'' problem across different filtration scales. We propose a compact spectral representation that distills the persistent Laplacian into three mathematically grounded invariants: Betti numbers, the spectral gap, and analytic torsion. Across benchmark datasets including MNIST, QM-3D, and SKEMPI WT, we demonstrate that this reduced feature space captures the essential predictive signal of the full spectrum, and in some cases outperforms it, while significantly reducing computational overhead and preventing the noise introduced by higher-frequency eigenvalues. Our results suggest that these invariants provide a principled, fixed-length interface between spectral geometry and topological learning.

</details>


### [285] [From Tokens to Policy: Causal and Interpretable Heterogeneous Treatment Effects Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17010)
*Riccardo Cadei,Frank Otchere,Nyasha Tirivayi,Gustavo Angeles Tagliaferro,Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEXIS方法，将异质处理效应（HTE）识别重新定义为马尔可夫毯发现问题，利用多模态预治疗表征（如卫星图像）来发现未测量的潜在交互因子，从而实现对HTE的可解释因果表征，并在非洲两项反贫困项目中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HTE识别方法在表达性与可解释性之间存在权衡，且当某些活跃的异质性驱动因子未被测量时，无论采用何种方法都会产生虚假的HTE表征，缺乏因果解读。本文旨在利用更广泛的预治疗多模态/多视角测量数据和可扩展表征，实现可解释且具有因果意义的HTE识别。

Method: 将HTE识别重新框架为在充分且对齐的预治疗表征上的马尔可夫毯发现问题；提出神经暴露交互搜索（NEXIS）迭代算法，该算法具有可证明的一致选择性。

Result: 在两项非洲反贫困项目中部署NEXIS，通过引入卫星影像捕捉先前未测量的环境效应修饰因子，生成了新颖、可解释且具有指导性的优化建议。

Conclusion: 通过结合多模态预治疗测量与NEXIS方法，可以在受控实验中实现具有因果解读的HTE表征，为政策优化提供可解释的处方性指导。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Treatment Effect (HTE) identification is crucial to explain the impact of an intervention and optimize our policies accordingly. Existing approaches trade expressivity for interpretability, but, if some active heterogeneity drivers are unmeasured, methods at both ends of this spectrum allow for spurious HTE characterization with no causal reading. In this work, we focus on controlled experiments and argue that an oracle HTE causal characterization via the latent interactors is now within reach, thanks to (i) more extensive pre-treatment measurements, i.e., multi-modal and multi-view, and (ii) scalable representations with minimal human supervision. We then re-frame HTE identification as a Markov-blanket discovery problem on a sufficient and aligned pre-treatment representation, and introduce Neural EXposure Interaction Search (NEXIS), an iterative procedure with provable and empirically validated consistent selection. We deploy NEXIS on two anti-poverty programs in Africa, augmenting each with satellite imagery capturing previously unmeasured environmental effect modifiers, leading to novel, interpretable and prescriptive guidelines to optimize the programs' next iterations.

</details>


### [286] [Filtered Conformal Ellipsoids for Graph-Native Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17014)
*Yannick Limmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于过滤共形椭球的多变量时间序列联合预测方法，通过冻结状态空间滤波器生成一步预测均值和协方差，再利用分裂共形校准调整标量半径，从而在不依赖高斯尾部概率的情况下实现覆盖控制。针对滤波后分数存在依赖且循环滤波器不收缩的问题，文章在可观测预测律商空间下分析了收缩性，并建立了Chebyshev型和Bernstein型近似覆盖界。在METRLA-20和PEMSBAY-50等图原生交通数据集上，该方法优于静态协方差和非滤波基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列的联合预测集需要同时控制单一事件并适应跨坐标的依赖关系。现有方法要么依赖高斯假设（如椭球基于高斯分位数），要么难以处理序列依赖和滤波器的非收缩性问题。本文旨在设计一种既能利用学习到的预测协方差（通过滤波捕捉序列依赖）又能保证覆盖保证（通过共形校准）的联合预测集构造方法。

Method: 采用冻结状态空间滤波器（如GCN-GRU）生成一步预测均值和协方差，构造马氏距离分数；通过分裂共形校准（split-conformal calibration）学习标量半径，形成椭球预测集。理论分析上，引入可观测预测律商（observable predictive-law quotient）识别产生相同未来高斯律的隐状态，在稳定贝叶斯高斯投影滤波器、协方差界和有限视界可观测性Fisher条件下，将收缩性转化为高斯负对数似然的控制；结合阈值自协方差包络得到Chebyshev型覆盖界，附加几何混合浓度假设得到Bernstein型界。在实现在GCN-GRU滤波器中采用对角加低秩协方差结构。

Result: 在METRLA-20和PEMSBAY-50等图原生交通数据集上，学习的滤波椭球比静态协方差和非滤波基线更尖锐（更小的体积）。在全图规模和非图原生数据集上，因子模型和Copula基线表现更强。

Conclusion: 提出的过滤共形椭球框架能有效结合滤波器的序列建模能力和共形校准的分布无关覆盖保证，在强依赖场景下获得更紧的预测集。但方法依赖于滤波器的可控性，在非图原生或大规模场景下优势减弱。

Abstract: Joint prediction sets for multivariate time series should control a single event while adapting to cross-coordinate dependence. We study filtered conformal ellipsoids: a frozen state-space filter emits a one-step predictive mean and covariance, and split-conformal calibration is applied to the resulting Mahalanobis scores. The filter is used to choose the ellipsoid shape; conformal calibration chooses the scalar radius, so the construction benefits from a learned predictive covariance without relying on Gaussian tail probabilities for coverage. The main difficulty is that filtered scores are dependent and learned recurrent filters need not contract in their raw hidden state; we therefore analyse contraction in an observable predictive-law quotient that identifies hidden states producing the same future sequence of emitted Gaussian laws. Under a stable Bayes Gaussian-projection filter, covariance bounds, and a finite-horizon observability Fisher condition, small excess Gaussian negative log-likelihood implies contraction of the learned emitted laws. Combined with a threshold-autocovariance envelope this yields a Chebyshev-type approximate coverage bound for filtered split-conformal prediction under dependence; a sharper Bernstein-type bound requires an additional geometric-mixing concentration assumption. Under Gaussian oracle realisability we also obtain a near-oracle log-volume comparison within the class of conditionally valid Gaussian ellipsoid rules. We instantiate the framework with a GCN-GRU filter with diagonal-plus-low-rank covariance. On moderate-size graph-native traffic benchmarks (METRLA-$20$ and PEMSBAY-$50$), the learned filter gives sharper at-target ellipsoids than static-covariance and non-filter baselines; at full-graph scale and on non-graph-native datasets, factor and copula baselines can be stronger.

</details>


### [287] [ExpRL: Exploratory RL for LLM Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17024)
*Violet Xiang,Amrith Setlur,Chase Blagden,Nick Haber,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpRL方法，利用人类编写的问答数据作为奖励支架（reward scaffolds）进行基于强化学习的中间训练，以提升大语言模型的数学推理能力。ExpRL将参考答案隐藏，仅用于构建评分标准，对策略模型生成的推理轨迹进行密集奖励评估，相比SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏方法，在数学推理任务上表现出更强的RL预训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏奖励RL提升LLM推理能力的效果严重依赖基座模型的覆盖范围，实践中通常需要手动设计中间训练（mid-training）来教授原始技能（如分解、验证、自我修正），但这种方法需要人工指定学习内容，且难以应对需要组合多种技能的复杂问题。因此，作者希望探索一种更自动化的基于RL的中间训练方法。

Method: ExpRL方法使用大规模人类编写的问答数据，但不将参考答案作为模仿目标，而是作为奖励支架：参考答案对策略隐藏，仅用于构建问题特定的评分标准，由LLM裁判将策略生成的推理轨迹与参考答案进行比较，分配结果级或过程级密集奖励。策略模型从原始问题提示中采样，而非从参考答案学习。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数学推理任务上，ExpRL相比SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏方法，提供了更强的RL预训练效果，并为后续的稀疏奖励RL提供了更好的初始化。额外的混合领域实验表明ExpRL可以扩展到纯数学场景之外。

Conclusion: 利用参考答案作为奖励支架而非模仿目标，通过密集奖励强化部分进展、有用的中间归约和富有成效的推理行为，可以更有效地进行RL中间训练，提升LLM的复杂推理能力。

Abstract: Sparse reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard tool for improving LLM reasoning, but its success depends critically on the coverage present in the base model. In practice, models are often primed for RL through \emph{mid-training} on curated reasoning traces that teach useful primitive skills such as decomposition, verification, or self-correction. Although effective, this strategy requires manually specifying what the model should learn, and it remains unclear whether such primitive coverage is enough for much harder problems, which require combining these skills into broader solution strategies. We study a more automated approach: \emph{RL-based mid-training} using large corpora of human-written question-answer data. Rather than treating reference solutions as targets to imitate, our method, ExpRL, uses them as \emph{reward scaffolds}: references are hidden from the policy and used only to construct problem-specific grading rubrics for judging on-policy reasoning traces. The policy samples from the original problem prompt, while an LLM judge compares the sampled reasoning trace against the reference solution and assigns outcome-level or process-level dense rewards. This lets ExpRL reinforce partial progress, useful intermediate reductions, and productive reasoning behaviors that sparse final-answer rewards often fail to upweight. On challenging math reasoning tasks, ExpRL yields stronger RL priming than SFT, sparse-reward GRPO, and self-distillation, and provides a better initialization for subsequent sparse-reward RL. Additional mixed-domain experiments further suggest that ExpRL can extend beyond the original math-only setting.

</details>


### [288] [HAMON: Passive Optical Sequence Mixing for Long-Horizon Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17028)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HAMON是一种被动衍射光学预测核心，通过光学传播直接进行时间序列预测，无需可训练的数字序列混合层。在多个标准基准上，它超越或接近最强数字基线，特别是在ETTm2和ETTh2数据集上MSE提升最高达14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准时间序列预测基准可能不需要Transformer等架构的密集叠加表示，核心预测算子往往具有低复杂度和近似线性特性。这引发了一个底层问题：如果核心预测算子如此简单，是否需要用学习的数字时序混合来实现？

Method: 将历史值编码到光学孔径上，未来位置保持暗区，通过级联可训练相位掩模和自由空间衍射直接在输出场中形成预测。推理时仅需一次被动光学传播，无需可训练的数字序列混合层。

Result: 在ETTm2上所有预测长度均优于最强数字基线，在ETTh2上除最长预测长度外均最优，MSE提升最高14%。在Weather上具有竞争力，在高通道数Traffic和Electricity数据集上落后于最强基线。

Conclusion: 消融实验和跨模拟器验证表明预测源自数据承载的光场而非数字预测头。该被动核心基于标准傅里叶光学，为光学硬件和被动物理序列混合提供了具体目标。

Abstract: Simple linear and frequency-domain models remain surprisingly competitive in long-horizon time-series forecasting, and recent mechanistic evidence suggests that standard forecasting benchmarks may not require the dense superposed representations that make transformers powerful in other domains. This raises a substrate-level question: if the core forecasting operator is often low-complexity and approximately linear, does it need to be implemented as learned digital temporal mixing? We introduce HAMON, a passive diffractive optical forecasting core in which historical values are encoded onto an optical aperture, future positions are left dark, and cascaded trainable phase masks with free-space diffraction shape the forecast directly in the output field. At inference, prediction is performed by a single passive optical propagation pass with no trainable digital sequence-mixing layer.
  Across standard benchmarks, HAMON outperforms the strongest digital baselines considered on ETTm2 at all horizons and on ETTh2 at all but the longest horizon, improving MSE by up to 14\% and doing so consistently across horizons rather than at isolated points. It is competitive on Weather and trails the strongest baselines on the remaining ETT settings and on the high-channel-count Traffic and Electricity datasets. Phase encoding, intensity-compatible readout, and phase-scrambling ablations, together with a TorchOptics cross-simulator check, indicate that the forecasts arise from the data-bearing optical field rather than from a digital forecasting head. Because the passive core uses standard Fourier optics, HAMON defines a concrete target for optical hardware and for passive physical sequence mixing.

</details>


### [289] [Your Privacy My Cloak: Backdoor Attacks on Differentially Private Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17035)
*Xiaolin Li,Ning Wang,Ninghui Li,Wenhai Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了差分隐私(DP)天然增强联邦学习(FL)后门攻击鲁棒性的假设，发现DP会掩盖恶意更新的统计特征导致现有防御失效，并提出利用DP掩蔽效应来最大化攻击影响的新型攻击RING，在中等隐私预算下对六种防御达到平均90.3%的攻击成功率，揭示了DP-FL中的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"差分隐私天然增强联邦学习对后门攻击鲁棒性"这一普遍假设，揭示DP与FL防御之间的根本张力。

Method: 通过实证分析两种基线攻击策略发现DP的掩蔽效应，提出RING攻击：利用DP掩盖恶意更新，通过协作构建对抗性扰动在聚合阶段重建强后门信号而不触发异常检测，该扰动层可兼容现有后门攻击。

Result: 在四个图像和文本数据集、非独立同分布设置下，RING在中等隐私预算下对六种最优防御达到平均90.3%攻击成功率，比基线策略提升最高26.08倍。

Conclusion: DP-FL存在根本性安全漏洞，缓解该威胁会带来显著的效用权衡，现有防御难以在不牺牲模型效用的情况下有效抵御此类攻击。

Abstract: Prior research suggests that differential privacy (DP) inherently enhances the robustness of federated learning (FL) against backdoor attacks. In this paper, we challenge this assumption. Through an empirical analysis of two baseline attack strategies, we uncover a fundamental tension in DP-FL: while bypassing DP allows state-of-the-art defenses to detect and filter malicious updates, complying with DP inadvertently masks their distinguishing statistical characteristics. Consequently, existing defenses become ineffective as DP reduces the raw backdoor signal. Building on this masking effect, we propose RING, a novel attack that explicitly exploits DP to conceal malicious contributions while maximizing attack impact. By collaboratively crafting adversarial perturbations, compromised clients reconstruct a strong backdoor signal during aggregation without triggering anomaly detection. RING operates as a perturbation layer that is agnostic to the underlying backdoor technique, making it broadly applicable and composable with existing attacks -- a property that significantly amplifies the threat it poses to DP-FL. Extensive evaluations across four image and text datasets under non-iid distributions show that RING achieves an average attack success rate of 90.3% against six state-of-the-art defenses under a moderate privacy budget, an improvement of up to 26.08x over baseline strategies. Finally, we evaluate potential countermeasures and find that mitigating this threat incurs significant utility trade-offs, exposing a fundamental security gap in the deployment of differentially private FL.

</details>


### [290] [Exact Posterior Score Estimation for Solving Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17048)
*Abbas Mammadov,Ozgur Kara,Kaan Oktay,Iskander Azangulov,Adil Kaan Akan,Hyungjin Chung,James Matthew Rehg,Yee Whye Teh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EPS方法，推导了线性高斯逆问题后验得分的闭式解，将后验采样转化为特定偏移下的去噪问题，并在多个数据集和逆问题上取得SOTA结果，同时比基于梯度的后验采样器减少约一个数量级的去噪器评估次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流模型通过训练去噪器逆转高斯噪声来学习强大的数据先验。在利用该先验求解线性逆问题时，需要从后验分布中采样，但先验提供的是无条件得分而非后验得分。现有方法要么用近似测量匹配校正来引导固定预训练去噪器，要么训练条件恢复模型而放弃先验的去噪结构，缺乏精确且高效的后验采样方法。

Method: 推导了线性高斯逆问题在后验得分上的闭式解，证明后验采样可简化为在算子依赖的偏移点和各向异性噪声协方差下的去噪问题。基于该恒等式提出EPS（Exact Posterior Score）训练目标，保持标准预训练的输入/输出结构，可从零训练或从预训练去噪器微调。推理时使用与底层骨干相同的采样器，无需似然梯度或投影。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet数据集上的五个线性逆问题（去噪、超分辨率、修复、压缩感知等）中，EPS在保真度、感知质量和分布指标上均优于无训练和有训练的基线方法，且使用的去噪器评估次数比基于梯度的后验采样器少约一个数量级。

Conclusion: EPS通过精确后验得分公式，将后验采样转化为标准去噪任务，实现了高性能、高效率、易于训练/微调的后验采样方案，为扩散/流模型在逆问题中的应用提供了新的理论框架和实践方法。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based models learn powerful data priors by training a denoiser to reverse Gaussian corruption. To use this prior to solve a linear inverse problem, one needs to sample from the posterior, but the score that the prior provides is the unconditional score, not the posterior score. Existing methods either steer a fixed pretrained denoiser with approximate measurement-matching corrections, or train a conditional restoration model that abandons the denoising structure of the prior. We derive the exact posterior score in closed form for linear Gaussian inverse problems under general Gaussian interpolants, and show that posterior sampling reduces to a denoising problem at an operator-dependent shifted pivot under an anisotropic noise covariance. We turn this identity into Exact Posterior Score (EPS), a denoising training objective that preserves the input/output structure of standard pretraining and can therefore be trained from scratch or fine-tuned from a pretrained denoiser. At inference, EPS uses the same sampler as the underlying backbone, with no likelihood gradients or projections. We evaluate EPS on five linear inverse problems across FFHQ and ImageNet, where it outperforms training-free and training-based baselines on fidelity, perceptual, and distributional metrics, while using roughly an order of magnitude fewer denoiser evaluations than gradient-based posterior samplers.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [291] [Semiclassical Gravity Efficiently Solves $\mathsf{NP}$-Complete Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14806)
*Matthew Fox,Chaitanya Karamchedu,Sotirios Mygdalas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，在经典引力场通过半经典爱因斯坦场方程与量子场耦合的假设下，一个非相对论性大质量量子比特的弱场动力学可以在多项式时间内解决NP完全问题，这表明该假设违反了物理扩展丘奇-图灵论题，从而间接支持了引力量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探讨经典引力与量子场耦合的半经典假设是否会导致超越标准量子计算能力的计算优势，从而检验物理扩展丘奇-图灵论题的有效性，并为引力量子化提供理论证据。

Method: 作者假设引力场是经典的，并通过半经典爱因斯坦场方程与量子场耦合，在此框架下分析一个质量大且非相对论性的量子比特（qubit）在弱场极限下的动力学行为，推导其是否能用于在多项式时间内解决NP完全问题。

Result: 结果表明，在所述假设下，该量子比特系统确实具备在多项式时间内求解NP完全问题的能力，这种强大的计算能力源于半经典爱因斯坦场方程所引入的非线性动力学。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：如果上述假设成立，则物理扩展丘奇-图灵论题将被违反，这反过来意味着经典引力与量子场耦合的半经典图像在物理上不可接受，从而支持引力的本质应是量子化的。

Abstract: Assuming the gravitational field is classical and that it couples to quantum fields via the semiclassical Einstein field equations, we show that the weak-field dynamics of a massive and non-relativistic qubit can in principle be used to solve an $\mathsf{NP}$-complete problem in polynomial time. We attribute this vast computational power to the non-linear dynamics afforded by the semiclassical Einstein field equations. Consequently, the above two assumptions entail a violation of the Physical Extended Church--Turing Thesis, which we regard as evidence for the quantization of gravity.

</details>


### [292] [Perturbative and Non-Perturbative Contributions to Black Hole Thermodynamics with String Clouds and Dark Matter Backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14815)
*Kumar Sambhav Upadhyay,Sudhaker Upadhyay,Bhabani Prasad Mandal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在完美流体暗物质背景和弦云环境下，黑洞热力学在反德西特时空中受微扰和非微扰量子修正的影响。从Bekenstein-Hawking熵出发，加入热涨落的修正，分别推导了对数修正熵和指数修正熵，并计算了质量、自由能、热容等热力学量。通过热容符号分析系统稳定性，发现非微扰效应对大黑洞可忽略，但对小尺度黑洞显著。两种修正均未在物理允许域内发现类范德瓦尔斯临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在PFDM背景和CoS环境下，微扰（对数）和非微扰（指数）量子修正对黑洞热力学性质及稳定性的影响，揭示不同修正机制在调控黑洞相结构中的对比作用。

Method: 基于Bekenstein-Hawking熵，分别引入小热涨落的微扰对数修正和非微扰指数修正，系统计算修正后的质量、亥姆霍兹自由能、吉布斯自由能、热容和压力等热力学量，并通过热容符号和状态方程分析稳定性及临界点。

Result: 热容符号分析表明系统存在从热力学不稳定到稳定的相变；非微扰效应对大黑洞可忽略，但在视界半径趋向普朗克尺度时变得显著；两种修正均未在物理允许域内发现类范德瓦尔斯临界点。

Conclusion: 对数修正和指数修正对PFDM黑洞的热力学稳定性和相结构具有不同的调控作用，非微扰效应仅在极小黑洞尺度下重要，且该系统在两种修正下均不呈现类范德瓦尔斯临界行为。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of perturbative and non-perturbative quantum corrections on the thermodynamics of black holes immersed in a perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) background with a cloud of strings (CoS) in asymptotically anti-de Sitter spacetime. Starting from the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy as the semiclassical baseline, we incorporate two distinct classes of corrections arising from small thermal fluctuations about thermodynamic equilibrium. In the perturbative sector, we derive the logarithmically corrected entropy and systematically compute the resulting modifications to the mass, Helmholtz free energy, Gibbs free energy, heat capacity, and pressure. The stability structure of the system is analyzed through the sign behavior of the heat capacity, which reveals a transition from a thermodynamically unstable to a stable phase. In the non-perturbative sector, we introduce exponential corrections to the entropy and carry out a parallel analysis of all thermodynamic quantities. We demonstrate that non-perturbative effects are negligible for large black holes but become significant as the horizon radius shrinks toward the Planck regime. In both sectors, we investigate the equation of state and search for a van der Waals-like critical point by examining the simultaneous vanishing of the first and second pressure derivatives with respect to thermodynamic volume; no such inflection point is found within the physically admissible domain. Our results illuminate the contrasting roles of logarithmic and exponential entropy corrections in governing the thermodynamic stability and phase structure of PFDM black holes with a CoS.

</details>


### [293] [An inductive approach to $f(R)$ gravity and its application to extended sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14859)
*Federico Scali*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文回顾了一种基于太阳系物理归纳构建的f(R)引力模型，推导了扩展源下的修正牛顿势，并探讨其在星系动力学中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前序工作通过太阳系测试约束了f(R)模型的基本长度尺度，本文旨在批判性回顾该模型的构建过程，强调其普适性与可推广性，并进一步推导扩展源下的修正牛顿势，为星系动力学应用奠定基础。

Method: 在Schwarzschild背景上对修正爱因斯坦方程进行微扰求解，后验重构f(R)拉格朗日量；利用经典太阳系测试约束偏离广义相对论的基本长度尺度；推导扩展源产生的修正牛顿势，分析牛顿极限的一致性。

Result: 构建了一个由太阳系物理归纳出的新f(R)引力族；获得了扩展源下的修正牛顿势；验证了牛顿极限的一致性；指出该模型在星系动力学中的应用前景。

Conclusion: 该f(R)模型不仅通过太阳系测试的约束，其修正牛顿势在扩展源情形下具有一致性，展现出应用于星系动力学的潜力。

Abstract: In a previous work (Phys. Rev. D 110, 064042), a new family of $f(R)$ gravitational Lagrangians was constructed inductively using only Solar System physics. Specifically, the modified Einstein equations were solved perturbatively on a Schwarzschild background, and the corresponding Lagrangian was reconstructed a posteriori from the solution. Classical Solar System tests were then employed to constrain the fundamental length scale quantifying the deviation from general relativity. In the first part of this work, the construction of the model is critically reviewed, emphasizing the generality of the approach and the applicability to different modified gravity frameworks. In the second part, the modified Newtonian potential produced by an extended source is derived within the new inductive $f(R)$ model, discussing the consistency of the Newtonian limit. In this context, the application to galactic dynamics emerges as an intriguing perspective.

</details>


### [294] [Tidal Stripping of Matter Bound to the Secondary in Extreme Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14894)
*Sreejith Nair,Sayak Datta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端质量比旋近（EMRI）系统中束缚在恒星级次级天体上的环境物质（气体或暗物质）在LISA频段旋近过程中被潮汐剥离的引力波效应，发现即使环境质量仅约10⁻³ M⊙，累积的退相位也可超过1弧度，在亚太阳质量情况下可达O(10³) rad，忽略此效应会严重偏置EMRI参数推断，且潮汐剥离现象可探测次级天体周围亚AU尺度、行星到亚太阳质量的环境物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EMRI环境研究几乎只关注主黑洞周围物质，忽略了束缚在次级天体上的环境物质及其在旋近过程中被潮汐剥离对引力波信号的影响。

Method: 理论分析束缚在次级天体上的环境物质在LISA频段旋近过程中被潮汐剥离的动力学过程，计算其对引力波相位的高阶修正，并通过与恒定质量波形模板的残差相位比较来量化剥离效应。

Result: 环境质量仅10⁻³ M⊙时累积退相位可>1 rad；亚太阳质量次级天体退相位可达O(10³) rad；忽略此效应会以频带内束缚质量相对变化量为量级偏置EMRI参数。

Conclusion: 潮汐剥离效应携带了束缚物质质量和致密性的信息，可用于探测围绕恒星级黑洞的亚AU尺度、行星到亚太阳质量的环境物质。

Abstract: Environmental studies of extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) have focused almost entirely on matter surrounding the primary supermassive black hole. We instead consider matter bound to the stellar-mass secondary (e.g., gas or dark matter); which can be progressively tidally stripped during the LISA-band inspiral. This changes the bound mass of the inspiraling object, modifying the gravitational-wave (GW) phase at leading order in the secondary mass. Furthermore, as the signal interpolates from an initially dressed inspiral to a nearly bare one, it can produce a characteristic inflection in the residual phase with constant mass waveform templates. Even for an environmental mass $\sim 10^{-3}\,M_{\odot}$, the cumulative dephasing relative to in band initial bound mass waveform can be larger than unity. In subsolar mass cases, the relative dephasing can reach $O(10^3)\, \rm rad$. Neglecting this effect may bias inferred EMRI parameters at the level of the fractional change in the in-band bound mass. The tidal stripping phenomena carry information about the mass and the compactness of the bound matter, enabling probes of sub-AU, planetary- to subsolar-mass environments surrounding stellar-mass black holes.

</details>


### [295] [Eccentricity in Disguise? Insights from GW231123 and Numerically Simulated Binary Black Hole Merger Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15150)
*Koustav Chandra,Johann Fernandes,Akshita Mittal,Gregorio Carullo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: GW231123是一个总质量~250 M☉的黑洞双星并合引力波信号，其自旋参数被标准模板推断为极高（χ≥0.7），但本文发现该高自旋可能源于忽略了偏心率的波形模型误导；通过偏心数值模拟和环影分析，发现准球形模板仍更受数据青睐，但现有偏心率模型不足以可靠区分两种解释；种群先验分析确认该事件在已知双黑洞种群中具有异常性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW231123是迄今探测到的最大质量黑洞双星并合事件，其推断的极端自旋值（χ≥0.7）难以通过常规并合或层级并合机制解释，但现有波形模型可能因忽略并合-衰荡阶段的偏心率修正而误导了自旋估计，因此需要检验偏心率是否会导致高自旋的误判。

Method: 利用现有偏心数值相对论目录重新分析GW231123；对比偏心率模板与准球形模板的拟合优度；采用与二元无关的环影分析方法获取无偏的残骸性质估计；在种群信息先验下进行参数估计。

Result: 偏心率模板可提供良好拟合，但准球形模板仍更受数据青睐；环影分析确认存在与大偏心率值相关的次级似然峰；种群先验分析验证了该事件在黑双星种群中的异常性。

Conclusion: GW231123的高自旋推断可能受到忽略偏心率效应的影响，但现有偏心率模型不够完善，无法可靠判定其真实物理性质；未来需要改进的偏心率波形模型才能确认该事件的偏心率解释。

Abstract: GW231123 is a gravitational-wave signal originating from the merger of a black hole binary with total mass $\sim 250 M_{\odot}$, the largest ever detected by the LIGO-Virgo-Kagra Collaboration. Remarkably, under standard priors, the system features among the fastest-spinning binary components confidently measured in binary mergers, $ χ_{1,2} \gtrsim 0.7$ at $90\%$ one-dimensional credibility, according to the most accurate model employed. As typical binary mergers result in remnants with $χ\sim 0.7$, such spin values are challenging to obtain even from previous (hierarchical) mergers. These inferred properties rely on waveform models lacking eccentric corrections in the merger-ringdown stage. Here, we show that binaries retaining significant eccentricity up to merger can be misinterpreted as near-extremally spinning when non-circular corrections are neglected. Binary-agnostic ringdown analysis instead provides unbiased estimates of the remnant properties, provided that a robust estimate of the signal peak can be obtained. We re-analyse GW231123 using available eccentric numerical-relativity catalogues, finding that although eccentric templates can provide a good fit to the data, quasi-spherical templates are still favoured. Ringdown analyses confirm a secondary likelihood peak correlated with large eccentricity values, but improved eccentric models will be required to assess the reliability of this interpretation. Finally, analysing GW231123 under population-informed parametric priors confirms the exceptional nature of this event within the current black hole binary population.

</details>


### [296] [General Relativity and Background Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15224)
*Antonio Vassallo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了经典时空物理学中背景独立性的多种形式化策略，论证背景独立性应被视为诊断和比较工具而非必然物理要求，并探讨了量子引力理论是否应继承广义相对论的背景独立性这一开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 背景独立性被认为是广义相对论的核心概念特征，但其精确的物理和哲学意义仍存在争议，需要系统的分析来澄清其含义和适用范围。

Method: 通过系统分析经典时空物理学中捕捉背景独立性的多种策略，并结合当代争论和案例研究进行论证。

Result: 背景独立性最好被理解为一种诊断和比较工具，而非必要的物理要求；消除背景结构既有概念上的优点也有解释上的代价。

Conclusion: 背景独立性在当代基础研究中仍是一个开放问题，其概念优点和解释代价的权衡有助于理解为何这一概念持续引发争论。

Abstract: General Relativity (GR) is widely regarded as a paradigmatic example of a background independent theory, a feature often taken to mark a decisive conceptual advance over its Newtonian and special relativistic predecessors. Yet the notion of background independence admits multiple formalizations, and its precise physical and philosophical significance remains contested. This chapter offers a systematic analysis of the strategies that have been proposed to capture background independence in classical spacetime physics. The discussion then turns to a central open question: whether, and in what sense, a successor theory of GR -- such as a theory of quantum gravity -- should be expected to inherit GR's background independence. Drawing on contemporary debates and a range of case studies, the chapter argues that background independence is best understood as a diagnostic and comparative tool rather than as a necessary physical requirement. The resulting perspective highlights both the conceptual virtues and the interpretive costs of eliminating background structures, and helps to explain why background independence remains an open problem in contemporary foundational research.

</details>


### [297] [Cosmological Pseudo-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15227)
*Manghang Limbu,Pramod Kamal Kharel,Rohit Bhattarai,Kiran Adhikari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量宇宙学扰动在不同背景（de Sitter空间及膨胀/收缩FLRW宇宙）中的赝熵（pseudo entropy, $\mathcal{S}$）。赝熵是纠缠熵的一种推广，包含实部与虚部。在de Sitter空间中，$\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{S})$在视界退出后增长，受声速$c_s$调控，并在晚期饱和；膨胀加速和收缩减速背景也有类似饱和行为。膨胀减速和收缩加速背景则在早期出现大的$\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{S})$并在视界再进入后振荡。$\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{S})$在de Sitter和膨胀加速情形衰减至零，但在膨胀减速和收缩加速情形中形成亚哈勃振荡带。与纠缠熵、Krylov复杂度和Nielsen电路复杂度相比，赝熵能捕获隐藏的相位信息；在非饱和区其斜率是Nielsen复杂度的$\sqrt{2}$倍。与电路复杂度（饱和边界与$w$无关）不同，赝熵在过渡区对状态方程参数$w$敏感，是更精细的宇宙动力学信息论诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠熵虽能刻画量子关联，但在宇宙学扰动中无法捕捉相位信息。本文旨在引入赝熵这一推广概念，研究其能否揭示纠缠熵及其他复杂度度量（Krylov复杂度、Nielsen电路复杂度）所缺失的相位动力学，并评估其对不同宇宙学背景（de Sitter、膨胀/收缩FLRW）的区分能力。

Method: 对de Sitter空间中标量宇宙学扰动（声速$0.024 \leq c_s \leq 1$）以及膨胀/收缩FLRW背景（状态方程参数$w$可变）中的赝熵进行理论计算和分析。比较赝熵与纠缠熵、Krylov复杂度、Nielsen电路复杂度在不同动力学阶段（视界内、退出、再进入）的行为，重点关注赝熵实部$\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{S})$和虚部$\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{S})$的演化特征。

Result: (1) de Sitter中$\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{S})$在视界退出后增长，$c_s$控制其起始时刻，晚期饱和。(2) 膨胀加速和收缩减速背景中$\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{S})$同样饱和；膨胀减速和收缩加速背景中$\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{S})$早期很大，视界再进入后振荡——这是因为压缩（squeezing）冻结但压缩角未冻结。(3) $\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{S})$在de Sitter和膨胀加速情形衰减至零，在膨胀减速和收缩加速情形中形成密集的亚哈勃振荡带。(4) 赝熵可捕获隐藏的相位信息，其非饱和区斜率为Nielsen复杂度的$\sqrt{2}$倍。(5) 电路复杂度的饱和边界与$w$无关，而赝熵在过渡区对$w$敏感。

Conclusion: 赝熵作为纠缠熵的推广，能额外捕获压缩角演化带来的相位信息，在区分不同宇宙学背景（尤其是膨胀加速与膨胀减速等）方面优于纠缠熵和电路复杂度。赝熵对$w$的敏感性使其成为宇宙动力学更精细的信息论诊断工具。

Abstract: We study pseudo entropy $\mathcal{S}$, a recent generalization of entanglement entropy, for scalar cosmological perturbations in de Sitter space with sound speed $0.024 \leq c_s \leq 1$, and in expanding and contracting FLRW backgrounds with varying equation-of-state parameter $w$. In de Sitter space, $\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{S})$ grows after horizon exit while $c_s$ controls its onset and saturates at late times. A similar saturation occurs in expanding-accelerating and contracting-decelerating backgrounds. In contrast, expanding-decelerating and contracting-accelerating backgrounds show large early-time $\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{S})$ followed by oscillations after horizon re-entry. This happens because while the squeezing freezes, the squeezing angle doesn't. Unlike entanglement entropy, pseudo entropy possesses an imaginary part, $\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{S})$, as well, which can encode the relative phase. $\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{S})$ decays to zero in de Sitter and expanding-accelerating cases, but forms dense sub-Hubble oscillation bands in expanding-decelerating and contracting-accelerating backgrounds. Compared with entanglement entropy, Krylov complexity, and Nielsen circuit complexity, pseudo entropy captures otherwise hidden phase information; in the unsaturated regime, its slope is $\sqrt{2}$ times that of Nielsen complexity. Unlike circuit complexity, whose saturation bound is $w$-independent, pseudo entropy is sensitive to $w$ during the transition regime, making it a finer information theoretic diagnostic of cosmological dynamics.

</details>


### [298] [Probing $\boldsymbolΛ$CDM-mimicking $\boldsymbol{f(Q)}$ gravity model using gravitational waves from compact binary coalescences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15295)
*Sikha Gogoi,Umananda Dev Goswami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在f(Q)引力模型框架下，利用未来第三代地面引力波探测器（爱因斯坦望远镜ET和宇宙探索者CE）对引力波信号进行参数估计，以约束该模型中模仿ΛCDM背景的自由参数。结果表明，两个探测器都能在高红移下对该模型参数给出严格约束，展示了未来引力波观测检验广义相对论偏离的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波直接探测是物理学史上的重大成就，为检验广义相对论之外的物理偏差打开了新窗口。本文旨在利用未来第三代引力波探测器，对f(Q)引力模型中的自由参数进行约束，以探测引力波性质与广义相对论的潜在偏离。

Method: 考虑f(Q)引力模型框架下致密双星系统（包括双黑洞BBH、双中子星BNS、黑洞-中子星双星BHNS）的引力波信号修正，使用未来第三代地面引力波探测器爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）和宇宙探索者（CE）进行参数估计。

Result: ET和CE两种探测器均能在显著高红移下对f(Q)引力模型参数给出严格约束。

Conclusion: 未来引力波观测具有在f(Q)引力框架下检验引力波性质与广义相对论偏离的潜力。

Abstract: The direct detection of gravitational waves (GWs) is a very significant achievement in the history of physics and has opened a new window to probe the possible deviations of physics from that of general relativity (GR). In this work, we forecast constraints on the free parameter of an $f(Q)$ gravity model that mimics a $Λ$CDM background at the level of cosmic expansion. We consider modified gravitational wave signals from inspiraling of compact bianry systems such as binary black holes (BBH), binary neutron stars (BNS)and black hole neutron star binary (BBHNS) systems in the context of the $f(Q)$ gravity model and perform parameter estimation for two future third-generation ground-based GW detectors, namely Einstein Telescope (ET) and Cosmic explorer (CE), respectively. Our results show that both detectors can give tight constraints on the model parameter up to a significantly high redshift. These results show the potential of future GW observations to probe the deviations of the nature of GWs from that of GR within the framework of $f(Q)$ gravity.

</details>


### [299] [EMRI Dephasing from a Torsion-Inspired Near-Zone Kerr Deformation: Motivated by Spin-Polarized Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15381)
*Jingxu Wu,Liangyu Luo,Daniil Stepanenko,Jie Shi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究爱因斯坦-嘉当引力中自旋极化暗物质尖峰对极端质量比旋近(EMRI)的影响，发现扭转诱导的$1/r^3$局域扰动不会产生全局度规修正，而是引入质量重整化、对数尾项和$M/r^2$项。作者提出仅在近区作为匹配ansatz引入有效度规形变，并计算了圆轨道旋近和分析波形，发现理想绝热近似下可产生显著相移，但该预测具有乐观性，未包含完整探测器响应和耗散通量等效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI是探测强场区弱保守扰动的敏感探针，本文旨在研究爱因斯坦-嘉当引力中扭转诱导的自旋极化暗物质尖峰对EMRI的效应，为未来空间引力波探测器提供潜在的观测约束。

Method: 将自旋极化暗物质尖峰描述为Weyssenhoff流体，代数消去扭转后得到局域自旋贡献为$U_{tt}^{\rm spin}\propto-σ_0^2/r^3$。求解静态线性化场方程发现不产生全局$1/r^3$度规扰动，而是得到质量重整化、对数$r^{-1}$尾项和$M/r^2$项。引入有效度规$g_{μν}^{\rm eff}=g_{μν}^{\rm Kerr}+αh_{μν}^{\rm eff}$作为近区匹配ansatz，计算圆赤道面旋近和分析波形。

Result: 该扭转启发的形变模型在理想绝热计算中可产生较大的相移，但该预测是乐观的，未包含LISA/Taiji完整响应、Teukolsky/自洽通量、偏心率、轨道倾角或高维参数简并。

Conclusion: 结果应视为对有效近区算符的约束，而非最小耦合爱因斯坦-嘉当暗物质的预言。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) are sensitive probes of weak conservative perturbations in the strong-field region of massive black holes. We study a phenomenological EMRI model motivated by Einstein--Cartan gravity in which a spin-polarized dark-matter spike is described by a Weyssenhoff fluid. After torsion is eliminated algebraically, the local spin contribution contains a repulsive exterior source $U_{tt}^{\rm spin}\propto-σ_0^2/r^3$. Solving the corresponding static linearized field equation, however, does not produce a global $1/r^3$ metric perturbation; the response contains a mass renormalization, a logarithmic $r^{-1}$ tail, and an $M/r^2$ term. We therefore introduce $g_{μν}^{\rm eff}=g_{μν}^{\rm Kerr}+αh_{μν}^{\rm eff}$ only as a local near-zone matching ansatz, not as a complete rotating Einstein--Cartan black-hole solution. Within this torsion-inspired deformation we compute circular equatorial inspirals and analytic-kludge waveforms. The fiducial model can produce large phase shifts in an idealized adiabatic calculation, but the forecast is optimistic and does not include a full LISA/Taiji response, Teukolsky/self-force fluxes, eccentricity, inclination, or high-dimensional parameter degeneracies. The results should be read as constraints on an effective near-zone operator rather than as a prediction of minimally coupled Einstein--Cartan dark matter.

</details>


### [300] [The Quantum Boundary of Black Hole Interiors: Path-Integral Termination at Planck Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15423)
*Edward J. Shaya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 经典广义相对论预言黑洞中心存在奇点，但本文通过引力路径积分在普朗克曲率阈值处的行为，论证该奇点永远不会被到达。量子边界 $\mathcal{B}_Q$ 在有限正半径处截断时空流形，从而消除奇点。对于旋转黑洞，该边界能有效抑制质量暴涨不稳定性，并形成一个有限的宏观内禀作用量，表明经典奇点并非物理事件，而是几何的自然终端边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典广义相对论中黑洞中心奇点问题，论证该奇点在物理上永远不会被到达，避免引入超出半经典领域的跨普朗克自由度。

Method: 分析引力路径积分在普朗克曲率阈值处的支撑丧失；利用Sobolev嵌入定理证明爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量在普朗克曲率处的失效；结合因果退耦与几何信息捕获机制；对旋转黑洞的质量暴涨不稳定性施加量子几何截断；评估Gibbons-Hawking-York边界项在终端类空切片上的贡献。

Result: 引力路径积分在普朗克曲率阈值处失去支撑，形成量子边界 $\mathcal{B}_Q$，在有限正半径处截断时空；量子边界将旋转黑洞的质量暴涨放大倍数限制为 $n_{max} \approx 0.67\,(r_g/\ell_P)^{1/5}$（太阳质量最大自旋黑洞约 $10^7$ 倍）；边界上的GHY项给出有限内禀作用量 $S_{GHY}^{\mathcal{B}} \approx \frac{3}{2}Mc^2\,τ_\mathrm{evap}$。

Conclusion: 经典奇点并非物理事件，而是几何在量子效应下的自然终端边界；黑洞内部在普朗克曲率处被量子边界截断，无需引入跨普朗克新自由度即可在半经典领域内解决奇点问题。

Abstract: Classical general relativity predicts a singularity at the center of every black hole. We argue that this singularity is never reached. The gravitational path integral loses support at the Planck curvature threshold ($\mathcal{K} \sim \ell_P^{-4}$), forming a quantum boundary $\mathcal{B}_Q$ that truncates the spacetime manifold at a finite, positive radius ($r_\mathcal{B} \approx 10^{-22}\, \rm m$ for a solar-mass black hole). This suppression is driven by several reinforcing physical mechanisms: the mathematical Sobolev failure of the Einstein-Hilbert action at Planck curvature, as well as causal decoupling and geometric trapping of information.
  For realistic rotating black holes, we demonstrate that $\mathcal{B}_Q$ acts as a quantum-geometric cutoff for the mass inflation instability, capping the internal mass parameter at a finite amplification $n_{max} \approx 0.67\,(r_g/\ell_P)^{1/5}$ for a maximally spinning black hole ($\sim 10^7$ for a solar-mass), and dynamically enforcing a universal, sphericalized Schwarzschild-like core. Evaluating the Gibbons-Hawking-York boundary term over this terminal spacelike slice yields a finite, macroscopic interior action per boundary segment, $S_{GHY}^{\mathcal{B}} \approx \frac{3}{2}Mc^2\,τ_\mathrm{evap}$, entirely distinct from the exterior Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. Operating within the semiclassical domain without injecting novel trans-Planckian degrees of freedom, these results suggest the classical singularity is not a physical event, but the natural terminal boundary of the geometry.

</details>


### [301] [Traversable Casimir Wormholes with Gravitational Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15552)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Francisco Bento Lustosa,Celio R. Muniz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由修正引力记忆效应的Casimir源支撑的可穿越虫洞几何。通过将板间距推广为Morris-Thorne时空中的有效径向尺度，得到由负Casimir贡献（~r⁻⁴）和正记忆修正（~r⁻⁷）组成的密度分布。从Einstein方程导出形状函数，由恒定状态方程和喉部正则性确定红移函数，分析了曲率标量、嵌入结构、能量条件和TOV平衡。径向零能量条件在喉部必然违反，而切向部分依赖于红移梯度。解的阴影半径可与M87*的EHT观测范围重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力记忆效应能否修正Casimir支撑的虫洞几何，以及记忆参数如何影响虫洞的可穿越性和观测特征。

Method: 将Casimir腔中受引力记忆修正的真空极化有效源引入Morris-Thorne虫洞框架，通过板间距→径向尺度的推广构造密度分布，从Einstein方程求解形状函数，结合恒定状态方程和喉部正则性确定红移函数，分析能量条件和平衡方程。

Result: 获得了由负Casimir项（~r⁻⁴）和正记忆修正项（~r⁻⁷）组成的密度分布；记忆参数定义了Casimir主导区和类幻象区之间的过渡；径向NEC在喉部必然违反；解的阴影半径可与M87*的EHT观测匹配。

Conclusion: 引力记忆可以软化通常的Casimir贡献，改变近喉部几何并重塑维持可穿越性所需的内部应力平衡，为Casimir支撑虫洞的可观测性提供了新的理论窗口。

Abstract: We investigate a class of traversable wormhole geometries supported by an effective Casimir source corrected by gravitational memory. The construction is motivated by the fact that a time-dependent gravitational background can leave a permanent positive shift in the vacuum polarization of a quantum field confined to a Casimir cavity. By promoting the plate separation to an effective radial scale in the Morris-Thorne spacetime, we obtain a density profile composed of the usual negative Casimir contribution, proportional to $r^{-4}$, and a positive memory-induced correction, proportional to $r^{-7}$. The corresponding shape function is derived directly from the Einstein equations and satisfies the throat condition by construction. We determine the redshift function from a constant barotropic equation of state together with the requirement of regularity at the throat, which fixes the barotropic parameter in terms of the Casimir and memory coefficients. The flare-out condition defines the admissible range of the memory parameter and separates a Casimir-dominated sector from a phantom-like sector, with the transition point associated with a singular limit of the constant-barotropic description. We analyze the curvature scalar, the embedding structure, the energy conditions, and the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equilibrium of the anisotropic matter source. The radial null energy condition is necessarily violated at the throat, while the tangential sector depends on the redshift gradient. We also examine the shadow radius as a phenomenological diagnostic and show that admissible solutions can overlap the Event Horizon Telescope range for M87*. The results indicate that gravitational memory can deform Casimir-supported wormholes by softening the ordinary Casimir contribution, modifying the near-throat geometry, and reshaping the internal stress balance required to sustain traversability.

</details>


### [302] [Testing the Nature of Rotating Black Hole Shadows Surrounded by a Thin Accretion Disk within Rastall Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15727)
*Abdul Malik Sultan,Muhammad Israr Aslam,Manahil Ali,Dongping Su*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过射线追踪算法研究了Rastall引力中旋转黑洞的阴影和吸积特征，发现Rastall参数μ和结构参数γ对阴影半径、扭曲度和光子环形态有显著影响，其中增大γ会使阴影半径扩大、扭曲度降低，阴影边界更趋圆形，表明阴影观测可有效约束Rastall引力中旋转黑洞的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Rastall引力中旋转黑洞在多种光照环境下的观测外观（阴影与吸积特征），并分析时空参数对阴影形态的影响，旨在为约束该引力理论中的黑洞参数空间提供观测工具。

Method: 采用射线追踪算法，从观测者屏幕向发射区域追踪光子轨迹，模拟旋转黑洞的阴影和光子环形态，系统分析Rastall参数μ、结构参数γ和旋转参数a对阴影半径、扭曲度及光子环形态的影响。

Result: 固定旋转参数a时，阴影观测量对Rastall引力参数有显著依赖性；增大结构参数γ导致阴影半径逐渐扩大（光子捕获区域扩张），同时扭曲度下降（阴影边界更趋圆形）；Rastall参数μ也呈现类似修饰效应，μ和γ的共同作用在阴影形态上留下独特印记。

Conclusion: 阴影观测可作为有效工具约束Rastall引力中旋转黑洞的参数空间。

Abstract: We investigate the observational appearance of a rotating black hole (BH) in Rastall gravity by analyzing its shadow and accretion signatures under different illumination environments. The spacetime geometry is characterized by the Rastall parameter $μ$, the structure parameter $γ$, and the rotation parameter $a$. To visualize the BH environment, we employ a ray-tracing algorithm that follows photon trajectories from the observer's screen to the emission region. We analyze how the shadow radius, distortion, and photon ring morphology respond to changes in the spacetime parameters. For a fixed value of $a$, the shadow observables exhibit a pronounced dependence on the Rastall gravity parameters. In particular, increasing the structure parameter $γ$ leads to a gradual enlargement of the shadow radius, indicating an expansion of the photon capture region surrounding the BH. At the same time, the distortion parameter decreases, implying that the shadow boundary becomes progressively more circular and less deformed. These results suggest that larger values of $γ$ tend to suppress the asymmetry induced by rotation and enhance the apparent size of the shadow. Similar modifications are observed for different values of the Rastall parameter $μ$, demonstrating that the combined effects of $μ$ and $γ$ leave distinct signatures on the shadow morphology. Consequently, shadow observations may provide an effective tool for constraining the parameter space of rotating BHs in Rastall gravity.

</details>


### [303] [Quasinormal modes and excitation factors of a regular black hole with zero-point length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15785)
*Milena Skvortsova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了正则Jusufi-Singleton黑洞的ringdown现象，其非奇异核心由非局部T-对偶引力模型中的零长度参数控制。考虑了标量、电磁和Dirac扰动。零长度参数提升了有效散射势垒，导致振荡频率系统性增加，并在大部分参数范围内加快阻尼。高阶WKB结果与时域积分吻合良好。此外，计算了激发因子，显示出对新的长度尺度的平滑依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正则Jusufi-Singleton黑洞的ringdown特性，探索非局部T-对偶引力模型中的零长度参数对黑洞振荡和阻尼行为的影响。

Method: 采用高阶WKB近似方法分析标量、电磁和Dirac三种扰动模式，并通过时域积分进行交叉验证，同时计算了激发因子。

Result: 零长度参数提升了有效散射势垒，导致振荡频率系统性增加，并加快阻尼；高阶WKB结果与时域积分高度一致；激发因子对新长度尺度呈现平滑依赖。

Conclusion: 零长度参数对正则黑洞的ringdown有显著影响，且高阶WKB方法在该模型中具有良好的适用性。

Abstract: We study the ringdown of the regular Jusufi-Singleton black hole, whose nonsingular core is controlled by a zero-point length arising from a non-local, T-duality-inspired gravitational model. Scalar, electromagnetic and Dirac perturbations are considered. The zero-point-length parameter raises the effective scattering barrier and produces a systematic increase of the oscillation frequencies, while also making the damping faster over most of the parameter range. High-order WKB results are checked against time-domain integration and show very good agreement for the dominant modes. We also compute excitation factors, which characterize the source-independent strength of the quasinormal-mode poles and show a smooth dependence on the new length scale.

</details>


### [304] [Geodesics Structure and Light Deflection of Regular Phantom Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15894)
*B. Malekolkalami,M. Haditale*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文分析了规则幻影黑洞(RPBH)在三种渐进时空(平坦、dS、AdS)中的测地线结构，研究了尺度参数b对粒子轨迹的影响，并通过有效势讨论了圆形轨道的稳定性，以及在渐进平坦时空中呈现了光线偏转角与尺度参数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究规则幻影黑洞的测地线结构及其在不同渐进时空下的行为，理解尺度参数b对粒子动力学的影响机制。

Method: 采用有效势(EP)工具分析圆形轨道及其稳定性；研究三种渐进时空间情况(Flat、dS、AdS)；分析尺度参数b对粒子轨迹的影响，该参数可模拟排斥或吸引效应。

Result: 尺度参数b能模拟粒子间的排斥或吸引效应；在渐进平坦时空中给出了光线偏转角与尺度参数b的关系。

Conclusion: 规则幻影黑洞的测地线结构强烈依赖于时空的渐进类型及尺度参数b，b可调控粒子动力学行为并影响光线偏转。

Abstract: The geodesic structure of Regular Phantom Black Holes (\textbf{RPBH}) space--time is analyzed and discussed in three asymptotically cases: Flat, de Sitter (\textbf{dS}), and Anti--de Sitter (\textbf{AdS}). The impact of the important scale parameter $b$\footnote{This parameter determines the coupling strength between phantom field and gravity.} on the trajectory of particles is studied and investigated which can mimic repulsion or attraction. By virtue of Effective Potential (\textbf{EP}) tool, the circular orbits and their stability are discussed. Also, in the asymptotically flat spacetime, the angle of light deflection versus the scale parameter is presented.

</details>


### [305] [From information bounds to infrared gravity: implications of Sharma-Mittal entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15996)
*Abdelhakim Benkrane,Giuseppe Gaetano Luciano,Ahmad Sheykhi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Sharma-Mittal熵在黑洞热力学和涌现引力中的热力学与引力含义，证明了其与贝肯斯坦界兼容，导出了基于朗道尔原理的修正质量损失关系，并在熵引力框架下得到了修正引力定律，其中特定参数关系R/δ=3/2自然复现了MOND加速标度a₀，为连接黑洞热力学、信息论和红外引力修正提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Sharma-Mittal（SM）熵——这一同时涵盖Rényi和Tsallis统计框架的双参数广义熵——在黑洞物理和涌现引力中的热力学与引力含义，寻找黑洞热力学、信息论与红外引力修正之间的统一联系，并为暗物质现象提供新的解释视角。

Method: (1)检验SM熵的引力实现与贝肯斯坦界的兼容性；(2)将朗道尔原理融入黑洞热力学，推导基于单比特信息擦除的修正质量损失关系；(3)在Verlinde熵引力框架下，基于SM熵推导修正的引力和加速度定律，并分析其与牛顿引力及MOND理论的偏离条件。

Result: SM熵在适当极限下能一致插值于Rényi熵和贝肯斯坦-霍金熵之间；修正质量损失关系在小质量和大质量区域呈现依赖于参数的非平凡渐近行为；熵引力导出的加速度在大距离偏离牛顿预言，并在参数条件R/δ=3/2下自然复现MOND-like regime，建立了SM参数与MOND加速标度a₀的直接联系。

Conclusion: SM熵框架有潜力为黑洞热力学、信息论和红外引力修正提供统一链接，并为传统归因于暗物质的现象提供新见解。

Abstract: The Sharma--Mittal (SM) entropy provides a two-parameter generalization encompassing both the Rényi and Tsallis statistical frameworks. In this work, we investigate its thermodynamic and gravitational implications in the context of black hole physics and emergent gravity. Specifically, we examine the compatibility of the gravitational realization of the SM entropy with the Bekenstein bound and show that the corresponding framework consistently interpolates between the Rényi and Bekenstein--Hawking entropies in the appropriate limits. By incorporating Landauer's principle into black hole thermodynamics, we obtain a modified mass-loss relation associated with one-bit information erasure, exhibiting nontrivial parameter-dependent asymptotic behavior in both the small- and large-mass regimes. Furthermore, within Verlinde's entropic gravity framework, we derive modified gravitational force and acceleration laws induced by the SM entropy. We show that the resulting acceleration deviates from the Newtonian prediction at large distances and naturally reproduces a MOND-like regime for the specific parameter relation $R/δ= 3/2$. This condition establishes a direct connection between the SM entropy parameters and the MOND acceleration scale $a_0$. Our findings highlight the potential of the SM framework to provide a unified link between black hole thermodynamics, information theory, and infrared modifications of gravity, while offering new insights into phenomena traditionally attributed to the dark matter paradigm.

</details>


### [306] [Quasinormal Modes and Hawking Radiation of Black Holes with Primary Scalar Hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16125)
*Roman Konoplya,Oleksandr Stashko,Zdeněk Stuchlík*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了超越Horndeski引力中带有标量毛的渐近平坦黑洞的准正则模、灰体因子和霍金辐射，发现标量毛会在环降信号中留下特征印记，包括频率偏移、泛音重整和回声产生，并导致灰体因子出现共振隧穿效应，使霍金辐射呈现振荡频率依赖。同时证明了对应的裸奇点具有量子力学奇异性，不存在良好定义的动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越Horndeski引力中新发现的带有初级标量毛的渐近平坦黑洞解的动态性质，研究标量毛对黑洞环降特性、灰体因子和霍金辐射的影响，以检验此类修正引力理论的可观测信号。

Method: 计算准正则模谱（包括基模和高阶泛音）、灰体因子和霍金辐射谱，分析标量毛存在时这些物理量的变化特征，并研究裸奇点的量子力学性质。

Result: 标量毛导致准正则频率偏移、泛音重整和回声产生；基模受中等程度影响，高阶泛音对近视界变形高度敏感；灰体因子在某些参数区域呈现共振隧穿行为；霍金辐射率出现振荡频率依赖；裸奇点量子力学奇异且动力学定义不良。

Conclusion: 黑洞光谱学和霍金辐射可作为超越Horndeski引力的互补探针，标量毛留下的特征印记有望通过引力波和辐射观测加以检验。

Abstract: Recently, a new family of asymptotically flat black-hole solutions endowed with primary scalar hair has been discovered in beyond-Horndeski gravity. We study in detail the quasinormal modes spectra, graybody factors, and Hawking radiation of this class of black holes. We demonstrate that presence of primary scalar hair leaves characteristic imprints on the ringdown properties, shifts the quasinormal frequencies, inducing overtone rearrangements, and rise of echoes. While the fundamental modes associated with the light-ring are affected moderately, higher overtones are highly sensitive to the small near-horizon deformation produced by scalar field. In certain parameter regimes, the graybody factors exhibit resonant-tunnelling behavior, which leads to an oscillatory frequency dependence of the Hawking emission rate. Thus, both black-hole spectroscopy and Hawking radiation may provide complementary and distinctive probes of the beyond-Horndeski gravity. Additionally, we demonstrate that the corresponding naked singularites are quantum mechanically singular and do not admit a well-defined dynamics.

</details>


### [307] [Interaction of fluids described through relative motion and application to Bianchi type-I spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16268)
*Peter Mészáros,Daniel Račko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了两种流体之间一种新型相互作用的应力-能量张量，该相互作用取决于两种流体体积元的相对速度，并通过一个流体的粒子密度在另一个流体参考系中的测量来定义。作者还研究了该相互作用在微小各向异性假设下对Bianchi I型时空演化的影响，发现各向异性演化未发生质变，与宇宙模型兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流体相互作用模型大多不考虑流体间相对速度对相互作用的显式依赖，本文旨在建立一种新的相互作用形式，其中粒子密度在对方流体参考系中测量，从而引入对相对速度的依赖。

Method: 利用一个流体的粒子密度通过另一个流体4-速度场所诱导的垂直超曲面上的度规张量来定义相互作用量，推导出依赖于两个流体4-速度标量积的应力-能量张量，并在微小各向异性近似下应用于Bianchi I型时空。

Result: 成功推导出含相对速度依赖的应力-能量张量表达式；在Bianchi I型时空的微小各向异性演化中，各向异性的演化性质未发生定性改变。

Conclusion: 该相互作用形式在简单非平凡应用场景下与现有宇宙学模型兼容，表明该模型可用于进一步研究涉及相对速度的流体相互作用系统。

Abstract: We derive the stress-energy tensor for a pair of fluids with a novel form of interaction that depends on the relative velocity of volume elements of the two fluids. The interaction is described through quantities measuring the local particle density of one fluid through the metric tensor induced on hypersurfaces perpendicular to the 4-velocity field of the other fluid -- the particle density of one fluid measured in reference frames of volume elements of the other fluid, as opposed to the standardly defined particle density of a fluid measured in reference frames of its own volume elements. This introduces an explicit dependence of the stress-energy tensor on the scalar product of 4-velocities of the two fluids, which can be expressed through the relative physical speed of their volume elements. We also investigate the effect of the studied interaction on the evolution of Bianchi type-I spacetimes, under the assumption of small anisotropy. This represents the simplest nontrivial application of the studied form of interaction. The evolution of the anisotropy does not change qualitatively, which implies compatibility with models of our Universe.

</details>


### [308] [Gravitational waveforms from periodic orbits around Gauss-Bonnet black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16280)
*Yi-Han Huang,Sen Guo,Yu Liang,Lin Wen,Kai Lin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力理论中极端质量比旋进(EMRI)系统的引力波特征。通过周期轨道分类和半相对论波形建模，发现即使微小的Gauss-Bonnet耦合(α~0.1M²)也会在强场区域产生可测量的几何特征，表现为引力波谐波梳的刚性高频拉伸。Fisher信息分析表明，对于LISA等空间探测器，在信噪比ρ=20的四年观测中，可将耦合参数约束至σ_α~10⁻⁶ M²，且与轨道离心率几乎无简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI是未来空间引力波探测器探测强场引力的最有前景的信源之一。传统修正黑洞模型常受Fisher矩阵病态奇异性困扰，而4D EGB引力因其严格的线性几何耦合特性可以有效规避这一问题，为检验经典时空的拓扑偏离提供了理想框架。

Method: 通过整数分类法(z,w,v)对赤道周期轨道进行分类；采用半相对论波形建模分析引力波特征；运用Fisher信息分析进行参数估计精度和简并性评估。

Result: GB耦合α~0.1M²仅引起全局保守能量预算约0.2%的偏移，但短程排斥性EGB核心会严重改变强场旋进动力学，每轨道累积数弧度的可分辨宏观退相。波形表现为引力波谐波梳的刚性高频拉伸，是一种清洁且振幅独立的谱特征。Fisher分析给出σ_α~10⁻⁶ M²的约束精度，且与离心率几乎无简并。

Conclusion: 4D EGB引力为检验修正引力理论提供了理想平台，其独特的线性耦合特性避免了传统模型的奇异性问题。所发现的引力波谱特征有望被LISA、Taiji和天琴等空间探测器探测到，实现α参数的极高精度约束。

Abstract: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) constitute one of the most promising probes of strong field gravity for future space borne gravitational-wave observatories. As a representative higher-curvature extension of General Relativity (GR), four-dimensional Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (4D EGB) gravity is distinguished by its strictly linear geometric coupling. By this mathematical property, the pathological Fisher-matrix singularities that typically plague conventional modified black hole models are effectively evaded, thereby providing an ideal framework to test topological deviations from classical spacetimes. Through the classification of equatorial periodic orbits via an integer taxonomy $(z,w,v)$, it is demonstrated that even modest Gauss-Bonnet couplings ($α\sim 0.1M^2$) imprint measurable geometric signatures onto the zoom-whirl architecture. Although the global conservative energy budget is shifted by a mere $\sim 0.2\%$, the short-range repulsive EGB core severely alters the strong field whirl dynamics, whereby a resolvable macroscopic dephasing of several radians per orbit is accumulated. Through semi-relativistic waveform modeling, it is revealed that this temporal compression manifests as a rigid, high-frequency stretching of the gravitational-wave harmonic comb -- a clean, amplitude-independent spectral signature ideally suited for detection by LISA, Taiji, and TianQin. A rigorous Fisher information analysis confirms that for a typical four-year observation at a signal-to-noise ratio of $ρ=20$, the marginalized error on the EGB coupling can be tightly bounded to $σ_α\sim \mathcal{O}(10^{-6}) M^2$, with virtually negligible parameter degeneracy with the orbital eccentricity.

</details>


### [309] [Ultraviolet Structure of Real-time Gravitational Wave Linear Response in a Resonant Scalar Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16296)
*Han Lai,Atsuhisa Ota*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在闵可夫斯基背景下，研究了时变共振标量场中引力波的实时线性响应。通过Schwinger-Keldysh形式论和绝热正则化方案，提取了单圈响应的紫外结构，发现时间依赖背景会诱导额外的局域发散，这些发散可通过Weyl平方项、时变Ricci标量项和时变宇宙学常数对应的局域抵消项进行重整化。同时指出线性响应与tadpole应力张量的重整化在超越领先绝热阶时存在不匹配，但认为这种不匹配与固定背景的离壳性质有关，预期在背景壳上时会消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究时变共振标量场背景下引力波的实时线性响应，特别是紫外发散结构及其重整化问题，并探讨线性响应与tadpole应力张量重整化之间的潜在不匹配。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Keldysh形式论处理实时关联函数，发展适用于不等时关联函数的绝热正则化方案，提取单圈响应的紫外发散结构，并通过局域抵消项进行重整化。同时引入协变完备化处理共振模型，分析背景的壳上条件对重整化不匹配的影响。

Result: 领先发散重现了熟悉的□²h_{ij}结构；时间依赖背景诱导了正比于□h_{ij}、∂₀h_{ij}和h_{ij}的额外局域发散。这些发散分别由Weyl平方项、时变Ricci标量项和时变宇宙学常数对应的抵消项重整化。线性响应与tadpole应力张量的重整化在超越领先绝热阶时存在不匹配。

Conclusion: 线性响应与tadpole应力张量的重整化不匹配源于固定背景的离壳性质；当背景被处理为壳上时，这种不匹配预期会消失。

Abstract: We study the real-time linear response of gravitational waves in a time-dependent resonant scalar field in a Minkowski background. In the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism, we develop an adiabatic regularization scheme for unequal-time correlation functions and use it to extract the ultraviolet structure of the one-loop response. The leading divergence reproduces the familiar $\Box^2 h_{ij}$ structure, whereas the time-dependent background induces additional local divergences proportional to $\Box h_{ij}$, $\partial_0 h_{ij}$, and $h_{ij}$. These are renormalized by local counterterms associated with the Weyl-squared term, a time-dependent Ricci-scalar term, and a time-dependent cosmological constant. We also compare the renormalization of the linear response with that of the tadpole stress tensor and find a mismatch beyond leading adiabatic order in the present toy model. By considering a covariant completion of the resonance, we further argue that this mismatch is tied to the off-shell nature of the fixed background, and is expected to disappear once the background is treated on shell.

</details>


### [310] [Generalized Unimodular Gravity and Cosmological Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16378)
*Júlio C. Fabris,Alexander Yu. Kamenshchik*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视广义单模理论及其宇宙学意义，通过纯几何方式获得宇宙的常规物质组分，为暗物质研究提供新视角。背景和微扰分析表明，该理论可复现广义相对论的相应结果，但具有不同的物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视广义单模理论，探索其在宇宙学中的应用，尤其是从纯几何途径导出宇宙物质组分，为暗物质和暗能量问题提供新的理论视角。

Method: 采用广义单模理论的框架，通过纯几何方法推导宇宙的常规物质组分；进行背景和微扰分析，并与广义相对论的结果进行对比。

Result: 在广义单模理论框架下，可以从纯几何方式得到通常的宇宙物质组分；背景和微扰分析的结果与广义相对论一致，但解释有所不同。

Conclusion: 广义单模理论为宇宙学中的暗物质和暗能量研究提供了新的几何视角，尽管在观测结果上与广义相对论等价，但其不同的物理解释可能为理解宇宙的暗成分开辟新路径。

Abstract: The generalized unimodular theory is revisited and its consequence for cosmology is discussed. The usual matter components of the universe are obtained in a pure geometric way. This result gives a new perspective to the studies of the dark sector of the universe. A background and perturbative analysis are carried out, recovering the corresponding results obtained through the general relativity theory but with a different interpretation.

</details>


### [311] [Kinetic Theory of Cosmological Magnetogenesis at Second Order: A New Density-Gradient Source and Comparison with the Harrison Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16680)
*Bob Osano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导并比较了三种宇宙磁生成机制：Takahashi等人的汤姆逊散射速度差机制、新发现的光子密度对比度-质电速度差耦合机制，以及Harrison整体流机制。通过完整的动理学理论链推导，发现新耦合项贡献约为Takahashi结果的0.97倍，使散射机制总贡献提高约1.4倍。所有种子场均远超星系发电机阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙大尺度磁场的起源是未解之谜，现有理论（如Takahashi等人的散射机制和Harrison机制）对早期宇宙磁场的产生给出了不同解释，但缺少统一的动理学理论框架来完整比较这些机制的相对贡献和有效性。

Method: 从耦合的麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼方程出发，完整推导BBGKY层级、汤姆逊碰撞项、广义欧姆定律直至二阶磁感应方程的动理学理论链；在宇宙学扰动二阶下，识别出光子密度对比度δγ与电子-光子速度差(u_e-u_γ)^(1)耦合的新源项；使用CAMB v1.6.6在z=1100进行数值评估。

Result: 新密度对比度-速度差耦合项贡献约为Takahashi结果的0.97倍，使散射机制总磁场上限达到约Takahashi结果的1.4倍；Harrison机制在普朗克整体流极限下产生约5.7×10^(-24) G（1 Mpc尺度），当β≳2×10^(-3)时占主导；所有种子场均远超星系发电机阈值；欧姆定律修正项均受限于m_e/m_p≈5.4×10^(-4)，确认单流体近似精度优于0.1%。

Conclusion: 本研究首次在同一动理学理论框架下统一比较了三种宇宙磁生成机制，识别并量化了此前隐含的新耦合项，表明散射机制总贡献大于此前估计；所有机制产生的种子磁场均足以触发后期星系发电机放大效应，为宇宙大尺度磁场的起源提供了多重候选途径。

Abstract: We derive and compare three mechanisms of cosmological magnetogenesis: the Thomson-scattering velocity-difference mechanism of Takahashi et al.\ (2005), a new density-gradient source identified here for the first time, and the Harrison bulk-flow mechanism of Cembranos et al.\ (2020). Starting from the coupled Maxwell-Boltzmann equations, the complete kinetic theory chain is derived in a single document -- from the BBGKY hierarchy and Thomson collision term, through the generalised Ohm's law, to the second-order magnetic induction equation. The Ohm's law correction terms are each bounded by $m_e/m_p\approx5.4\times10^{-4}$, confirming the standard single-fluid approximation to better than $0.1\%$. At second order in cosmological perturbations, products of first-order scalar source vorticity, we identify a coupling between the photon density contrast $δ_γ\equiv δρ^{(1)}_γ/ \barρ_γ$ and the electron-photon velocity difference $(u_e-u_γ)^{(1)}$ that was implicitly present in previous treatments but never isolated. Numerical evaluation with CAMB~v1.6.6 at $z=1100$ shows that this term contributes at ${\approx}0.97\times B_{\rm Tak}$, giving a scattering-mechanism total ${\approx}1.4\times$ the Takahashi result. The Harrison mechanism at the Planck bulk-flow limit ($β<8.5\times10^{-4}$) yields $B\approx5.7\times10^{-24}$~G at 1~Mpc today and dominates for $β\gtrsim2\times10^{-3}$, mildly above the Planck limit. All seed fields exceed the galactic dynamo threshold by many orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [312] [The entropy of black hole under second-order deviation from equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16757)
*Wen-Tao Fu,Ming-Fei Ji,Yu-Sen Zhou,Li-Ming Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有分叉Killing视界的球对称背景在二阶扰动下动力黑洞的熵。利用高斯零坐标，我们微扰地研究了表观视界在二阶下的几何。在协变相空间形式中，为了探索物质场的贡献，我们引入了一种新的修正规范能量，并建立了熵的二阶变化与进入黑洞的能量通量之间的平衡律。我们表明，当入射物质满足零能量条件时，熵精确等于二阶表观视界的面积，并且熵的变化也服从第二定律。我们还讨论了当零能量条件被违反时面积律仍可能成立的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论中，黑洞熵通常与事件视界面积相关联，但事件视界具有全局因果特性，在动力学过程中难以准确定义。表观视界作为局部定义的对象，更适合描述动力学黑洞的热力学性质。本文旨在探讨在二阶微扰理论中，动力黑洞的熵是否仍由表观视界面积给出，并建立相应的热力学定律。

Method: 采用高斯零坐标（Gaussian null coordinates）对球对称背景进行二阶微扰展开，研究表观视界的几何结构；利用协变相空间形式（covariant phase space formalism）构造新的修正规范能量（modified canonical energy），建立熵的二阶变化与物质能量通量之间的平衡律。

Result: 当入射物质满足零能量条件（null energy condition）时，熵精确等于二阶表观视界的面积，且熵的变化满足第二定律。同时探讨了零能量条件被违反时面积律仍可能成立的可能性。

Conclusion: 在二阶微扰理论框架下，动力黑洞的熵可由表观视界面积给出，且服从第二定律，这为动力学黑洞热力学提供了更坚实的局部描述基础。

Abstract: We investigate the entropy of a dynamical black hole arising from second-order perturbations of a spherically symmetric background with a bifurcate Killing horizon. Using Gaussian null coordinates, we study the geometry of the apparent horizon perturbatively up to second order. Within the covariant phase space formalism, to explore the contribution of matter fields, we introduce a new modified canonical energy, and establish a balance law relating the second-order variation of the entropy to the energy flux entering the black hole. We show that the entropy is given precisely by the area of the apparent horizon at second order when the null energy condition holds for the infalling matter, and that the variation of the entropy also obeys the second law. We also discuss the possibility that the area law continues to hold when the null energy condition is violated.

</details>


### [313] [Non-linear stability of the matter dominated universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16879)
*David Fajman,Elliot Marshall*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值方法研究了具有Gowdy对称性的爱因斯坦-欧拉系统中，具有多方状态方程的爱因斯坦-德西特时空的非线性扰动，结果表明该时空在足够小但其他方面通用的扰动下是稳定的，这与尘埃模型中已知的不稳定性形成鲜明对比，揭示了爱因斯坦-欧拉方程的一个先前未知的稳定区域，对宇宙学有直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究爱因斯坦-德西特时空在不同物质模型（尤其是非尘埃的流体模型）下的非线性稳定性，以填补对该时空稳定性认知的空白。

Method: 对具有Gowdy对称性的爱因斯坦-欧拉系统进行数值模拟，选取多方状态方程，研究爱因斯坦-德西特时空的非线性扰动演化。

Result: 在足够小但通用的扰动下，爱因斯坦-德西特时空对于多方状态方程的流体是稳定的。这与尘埃模型下的已知不稳定性结果相反。

Conclusion: 发现了爱因斯坦-欧拉方程的一个先前未知的稳定区域，暗示了宇宙学模型中物质状态方程对时空稳定性的关键影响，为更现实的宇宙学模型提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We numerically study non-linear perturbations of the Einstein-de Sitter spacetime as a solution to the Gowdy-symmetric Einstein-Euler system for a polytropic equation of state. The results suggest that the Einstein-de Sitter spacetime is stable for sufficiently small but otherwise generic perturbations. This is in stark contrast to the well known instability of this spacetime when the matter model is dust. Moreover, this indicates a previously unknown stable regime of the Einstein-Euler equations with direct implications for cosmology.

</details>


### [314] [Fixed-quadrupole static tidal response of Schwarzschild black holes in a cubic Weyl effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16909)
*Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维Schwarzschild黑洞在奇宇称三次Weyl有效场论修正下的静态潮汐响应。通过构建约化径向作用量、降阶高阶导数方程并直接求解度规变量边界值问题，发现固定四极矩响应振幅为Δ(B/A) = -2400ε_e，标量固定-ℓ商为Δk_{2,sc}^{fix} = -20ε_e，并强调该结果需要与规范不变的Teukolsky-based Love数进行ℓ解析延拓和归一化映射后才能比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准广义相对论中Schwarzschild黑洞的静态Love数为零，但有效场论中的高阶引力修正（如三次Weyl算符）可能产生非零潮汐响应。研究旨在量化该修正对静态四极矩潮汐解的影响。

Method: 在ε_e = λ_e(Λr_s)^{-4}微扰展开下，构建静态偶宇称ℓ=2扰动的约化二次径向作用量，对高阶导数方程进行降阶处理，直接以度规变量求解边界值问题。通过视界正则性和无穷远匹配确定解，利用关联Legendre分支P₂²和Q₂²校准空间扇区，提取固定四极矩响应。

Result: 固定四极矩响应振幅Δ(B/A) = -2400ε_e，标量固定-ℓ商Δk_{2,sc}^{fix} = -20ε_e。指出该结果为度规扇区的可重复基准，需与规范不变的Teukolsky方法结果进行比较，并要求额外的ℓ解析延拓和归一化映射。

Conclusion: 三次Weyl有效场论修正下Schwarzschild黑洞的静态潮汐响应非零且可计算，但所得Love数并非直接对应规范不变的解析延拓电Love数，而是作为度规扇区基准，补充规范不变主方程方法。

Abstract: Static Love numbers of four-dimensional Schwarzschild black holes vanish in general relativity. We study how the fixed-quadrupole static tidal solution is modified by the parity-even cubic Weyl operator in the gravitational effective field theory. Working perturbatively in $ε_{\rm e}=λ_{\rm e}(Λr_s)^{-4}$, we construct the reduced quadratic radial action for static even-parity $\ell=2$ perturbations, order-reduce the higher-derivative equations, and solve the resulting boundary-value problem directly in metric variables. The order-$ε_{\rm e}$ equations reduce to a first-order two-dimensional inhomogeneous system for $X_0$ and $X_K$, with $X_2$ fixed by an algebraic constraint. Horizon regularity leaves one constant, but matching to infinity shows that this freedom only renormalizes the applied tidal branch. After removing this tidal renormalization, the decaying branch is unambiguous. Calibrating the spatial sector at fixed $\ell=2$ against the associated-Legendre branches $P_2^{2}$ and $Q_2^{2}$, we obtain a fixed-quadrupole response amplitude $Δ(B/A)=-2400ε_{\rm e}$. Equivalently, the scalar fixed-$\ell$ quotient gives $Δk_{2,\rm sc}^{\rm fix}=-20ε_{\rm e}$. The second number is a scalar fixed-$\ell$ conversion of the metric branch ratio, not, by itself, the analytically continued, gauge-invariant electric Love number. A comparison with canonical Teukolsky-based Love numbers requires an additional continuation in $\ell$ and a precise map of normalizations. The result should therefore be viewed as a reproducible metric-sector benchmark for the cubic Weyl EFT, complementary to gauge-invariant master-equation approaches.

</details>


### [315] [Electromagnetic Kantowski--Sachs Solutions in Teleparallel $F(T)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16982)
*Alexandre Landry*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在teleparallel F(T)引力框架下，利用CSC形式和Coley-Landry不变量方法，发展了电磁Kantowski-Sachs几何的协变重构理论。在Maxwell兼容分支中，电磁守恒定律约束了各向异性标度因子，导出ρ_em∝A_3^{-4}。通过幂律和指数ansätze重构F(T)函数形式，得到电、磁、横电磁扇区的不变量重构分支，并利用F_T>0和F_TT>0进行可行性筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展电磁Kantowski-Sachs几何在teleparallel F(T)引力中的协变重构框架，填补该领域在电磁各向异性宇宙学与黑洞内部类行为统一描述方面的理论空白。

Method: 采用CSC形式与Coley-Landry不变量方法，在Maxwell兼容分支中利用电磁守恒定律约束KS标度因子，推导对称与反对称场方程，通过幂律和指数ansätze重构F(T)函数形式，并利用不变量分类和F_T>0、F_TT>0条件筛选可行分支。

Result: 导出了ρ_em∝A_3^{-4}的电磁标度关系；获得了幂律和指数两种重构分支，分别对应电、磁、横电磁扇区；指数分支自然产生约化teleparallel de Sitter极限和平移模型F(T)=f(T_0-T)；重构分支描述了各向异性宇宙学扇区及局部BH内部类扇区，后者可再现RN-dS型行为。

Conclusion: 成功建立了电磁KS几何在F(T)引力中的协变重构框架，揭示了重构分支的局域性与分支依赖性，为理解各向异性宇宙学与BH内部动力学提供了统一的不变量分类方法。

Abstract: A covariant reconstruction framework for electromagnetic Kantowski--Sachs (KS) geometries in teleparallel $F(T)$ gravity is developed using the coframe/spin-connection (CSC) formalism and the Coley--Landry invariant approach. In a restricted Maxwell-compatible branch, the electromagnetic conservation laws strongly constrain the anisotropic KS scale factors and lead to the scaling $ρ_{\mathrm{em}}\propto A_3^{-4}$. The corresponding symmetric and antisymmetric field equations are derived and used to reconstruct the functional form of $F(T)$ directly from the KS dynamics. Power-law and exponential ansätze generate distinct invariant reconstruction branches associated with electric, magnetic, and transverse electromagnetic sectors. The exponential branch naturally admits reduced teleparallel de Sitter limits and shifted models of the form $F(T)=f(T_0-T)$. The reconstructed branches describe anisotropic cosmological sectors together with local BH-interior-like sectors that may reproduce reduced BH-interior-like or RN--dS-type behaviors at the level of the KS dynamics. These branches are organized through the invariant coframe/spin-connection classification and screened using the necessary leading-order viability conditions $F_T>0$ and $F_{TT}>0$. The local and branch-dependent nature of the construction is emphasized throughout.

</details>


### [316] [Inflationary branch decoherence and the cosmological arrow of time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21263)
*Ali Nayeri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文分析了暴胀量子宇宙学中分支退相干机制，通过计算约化密度矩阵和分支重叠因子，建立了量子宇宙学边界条件、暴胀压缩与经典宇宙历史涌现之间的定量联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暴胀过程中量子涨落如何演化为经典宇宙学历史，特别是不同量子宇宙学边界条件（Hartle-Hawking无边界态与隧穿态）下的分支退相干行为差异。

Method: 采用影响泛函形式推导轻场环境噪声核，基于视界和EFT粗粒化方案评估退相干；利用Bunch-Davies模函数计算单模分支重叠，在无质量极限下得到闭合解析形式。

Result: 在无质量极限下得到分支重叠因子 $|\mathcal{D}_k(z)|=[z^2/(z^2+1)]^{1/4}$；对于有质量场，超视界尺度上 $|\mathcal{D}_k(z)|\sim z^ν$。无质量情形下几何分支泛函的累积项包含主导的截断敏感相空间项和普适的次领头贡献。

Conclusion: 该计算为量子宇宙学边界条件、暴胀压缩与经典宇宙历史涌现之间建立了明确的定量桥梁。

Abstract: We analyze branch decoherence in inflationary quantum cosmology by computing reduced density matrices and branch-overlap factors for long-wavelength perturbations. The Hartle-Hawking no-boundary state is real in the semiclassical regime and contains both expanding and contracting WKB components, whereas the tunneling state is selected as an outgoing complex WKB branch; expanding-contracting decoherence is therefore central for the former and mainly diagnostic for the latter. Using the influence-functional formalism, we derive the noise kernel for a light spectator environment and evaluate decoherence under horizon-based and EFT-motivated coarse grainings. We then compute the single-mode branch overlap directly from the Bunch-Davies mode functions, obtaining $|\mathcal{D}_k(z)|=[z^2/(z^2+1)]^{1/4}$ in the massless limit and $|\mathcal{D}_k(z)|\sim z^ν$ on superhorizon scales for massive fields, where $z=-kη$ is the dimensionless wavenumber with $η$ the conformal time. In the massless case, the accumulated geometric branch functional is evaluated in closed form, with a leading cutoff-sensitive phase-space term and a universal subleading contribution. The calculation provides an explicit quantitative bridge between quantum-cosmological boundary conditions, inflationary squeezing, and the emergence of effectively classical cosmological histories.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [317] [Machine Learning-Driven Chemical Reactor Network Modeling of the Sandia-D Flame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14729)
*Nicolas J. Tricard,Benjamin C. Koenig,Sili Deng*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化机器学习辅助框架，用于构建湍流甲烷/空气火焰的等效反应器网络（ERN）。该方法结合主成分分析降维、k-means聚类初始化反应器网络，以及基于有限差分梯度下降的优化策略。在Sandia-D火焰的30个RANS模拟中，优化后的7反应器ERN实现了最大温度R²=0.7945，同时保持约6000倍于CFD求解器的加速比。尽管出口CO预测仍具挑战（R²=-0.4183），但较未经优化的初始聚类有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流燃烧模拟对科学和工程系统至关重要，但直接数值模拟因多尺度多物理行为的计算成本过高而通常不可行。ERN方法通过将多维湍流模拟替换为一系列0-D和1-D化学反应器来提高计算效率，但现有ERN构建方法要么依赖专家经验，要么自动化方法牺牲精度。因此，需要一种自动化且保持精度的ERN构建方法。

Method: 1）使用主成分分析（PCA）对高维热化学CFD数据进行降维；2）在低维潜空间中使用k-means聚类识别物理可解释的火焰区域，初始化反应器网络图；3）利用有限差分梯度下降（围绕不可微的Cantera反应器模拟）对初始网络进行优化。在30个不同工况（不同引燃温度与入口甲烷组分）的RANS模拟上验证。

Result: 7反应器ERN在最大温度预测上达到R²=0.7945，同时实现约6000倍于CFD求解器的加速比。出口CO预测的最终R²=-0.4183，但相比未优化的聚类初始化有显著改善。

Conclusion: 无监督热化学特征提取可为ERN构建提供有效的物理信息初始化，梯度基优化能在无需手动设计反应器网络的情况下显著提升预测精度。

Abstract: Turbulent combustion simulations are crucial for many scientific and engineering systems. However, the high cost to fully resolve the complex multiscale and multiphysics behavior makes direct simulation typically infeasible. The equivalent reactor network (ERN) approach attempts to improve computational efficiency by replacing a multidimensional turbulent simulation with a series of much cheaper 0-D and 1-D chemical reactors, providing a surrogate model that retains detailed chemistry at the cost of simplified flow physics. However, their development remains a challenge, often requiring either expert analysis, or automated approaches that sacrifice accuracy. In this work, we develop an automated machine-learning-assisted framework for constructing ERNs of the Sandia-D turbulent methane/air flame. Principal component analysis is first used to reduce high-dimensional thermochemical computational fluid dynamics (CFD) data to a low-dimensional latent space, where k-means clustering identifies physically interpretable flame regions used to initialize a reactor-network graph. This initialization is then refined using finite-difference gradient descent wrapped around non-differentiable Cantera reactor simulations. Across 30 RANS simulations spanning a range of pilot temperatures and inlet methane compositions, the optimized 7-reactor ERN achieves a maximum-temperature $R^2$ score of 0.7945 while preserving a $\sim6000\times$ speedup over the CFD solver. Outlet CO prediction remains more challenging, with a final $R^2$ score of $-0.4183$, but improves substantially from the unoptimized clustering initialization. These results show that unsupervised thermochemical feature extraction can provide effective physics-informed initializations for ERN construction, while gradient-based refinement can significantly improve predictive accuracy without manual reactor-network design.

</details>


### [318] [Distilling latent electrostatics from foundation machine learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15001)
*Xiaoyu Wang,Bingqing Cheng*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于潜埃瓦尔德求和(LES)的蒸馏方法，将基础机器学习原子间势(MLIP)转化为轻量级且具备静电响应能力的模型，通过从教师模型蒸馏能量和力来训练学生MLIP，可低成本提供玻恩有效电荷张量和红外光谱。实验表明，教师模型底层DFT水平和训练数据比架构本身对静电和光谱精度影响更大，对TiO2-水界面进行微调可改善预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础MLIP计算成本高且缺乏显式静电学描述，限制了其在长程相互作用和电响应主导系统中的应用。作者希望利用LES从DFT标签中提取隐式静电学信息，将基础模型转化为高效且具备电响应能力的轻量模型。

Method: 使用潜埃瓦尔德求和(LES)从教师基础MLIP预测的能量和力中提取隐式静电学，训练轻量级LES增强的学生MLIP，并可选择在额外DFT数据上进行微调。从UMA、MACE、Orb、eSEN、GemNet-OC、PET、EquiformerV2等多种基础MLIP蒸馏学生模型。

Result: 在液态水、浓盐酸和金红石TiO2(101)-水界面的红外光谱实验中，大多数基础MLIP均可提取静电响应。教师模型底层DFT水平与训练数据对静电和光谱精度的影响大于架构差异。对TiO2-水界面用适量高级DFT数据进行微调可改善结构和红外预测。

Conclusion: LES蒸馏方法是将基础MLIP转化为高效、具备电响应模型的实用途径，同时能够检验基础模型中所编码的物理保真度。

Abstract: Foundation machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have enabled atomistic simulations across broad regions of chemical and materials space, but many remain computationally expensive and lack explicit electrostatics, limiting their use for systems governed by long-range interactions and electrical response. Previously, we introduced Latent Ewald Summation (LES), which learns latent atomic charges and long-range electrostatics from density functional theory (DFT) energy and force labels alone. Here, we use LES to extract electrostatics that are latent in foundation models: energies and forces predicted by a teacher model are used to train a lightweight LES-augmented student MLIP, with optional fine-tuning on additional DFT data. The resulting models reduce computational cost while providing access to Born effective charge tensors, and infrared spectra. We benchmark student models distilled from a broad set of foundation MLIPs, including UMA, MACE, Orb, eSEN, GemNet-OC, PET, and EquiformerV2-based models, against experimental infrared spectra for liquid water, concentrated hydrochloric acid, and the anatase TiO2(101)-water interface. Across these systems, electrostatic response can be extracted from most foundation MLIPs. The benchmark further shows that the underlying DFT level and dataset used to train the teacher model play a larger role than architecture in determining electrostatic and spectroscopic accuracy. For the TiO2-water interface, fine-tuning with a modest amount of higher-level DFT data improves structural and infrared predictions. LES-based distillation therefore provides a practical route for converting foundation MLIPs into efficient, electrically responsive models, while also testing the physical fidelity encoded in foundation models.

</details>


### [319] [Atomic Design Transformer: xTB-Validated 3D Molecule Generation from Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15220)
*Takao Kotani*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种SE(3)等变自回归Transformer——原子设计Transformer（ADT），用于三维分子生成。通过将新原子位置编码在已放置原子的局部坐标系中实现SE(3)不变性。在QM9上表现有竞争力，在GEOM-Drugs上对7种骨架进行条件生成评估，XVR指标从11.1%到29.7%，生成速度快且化学多样性好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D分子生成方法在处理分子构型的旋转和平移等变性方面存在局限，且缺乏可靠的评估指标来验证生成分子在几何松弛后是否保持拓扑结构。作者旨在开发一种兼具SE(3)不变性、生成速度快、化学多样性高的实用3D分子生成模型。

Method: 采用自回归方式逐个放置原子，通过tokenization将新原子位置编码到先前放置原子的局部坐标系中，实现SE(3)不变性。网络架构为纯因果Transformer。引入xTB-Validated Rate (XVR)评估指标，使用xTB GFN2方法进行几何松弛后检查分子拓扑是否保持。

Result: 在QM9数据集上与最先进基线方法竞争力相当。在GEOM-Drugs数据集上，针对7种骨架的scaffold条件生成中，单个模型实现XVR从11.1%（吡嗪）到29.7%（苯）。生成分子化学多样性高，且由于纯因果Transformer架构，生成速度快。在50原子截断数据上XVR较低，但键和角的质量保持。

Conclusion: ADT作为一种实用的计算机辅助分子设计工具，兼具SE(3)不变性、快速生成能力和化学多样性，为虚拟筛选和分子设计提供了有效的候选分子生成方案。

Abstract: We present an SE(3)-invariant transformer for 3D-molecule generation, the Atomic Design Transformer (ADT). ADT places atoms one at a time, autoregressively. The SE(3) invariance is achieved by tokenization: each new atom's position is encoded in a local coordinate frame of a previously placed atom. The network is a plain causal transformer. For evaluation, we introduce xTB-Validated Rate (XVR), which checks whether the molecular topology is preserved after xTB GFN2 geometry relaxation. On QM9, ADT is competitive with state-of-the-art baselines. For GEOM-Drugs, we present a quantitative benchmark of scaffold-conditional 3D generation, evaluating seven scaffolds (benzene, pyridine, pyrimidine, pyrazine, furan, thiophene, cyclohexane) on the 30-atom-truncated ground-state GEOM-Drugs dataset. XVR ranges from 11.1% (pyrazine) to 29.7% (benzene), all from a single trained model. The generated molecules are chemically diverse, and generation is fast by virtue of the plain causal-transformer backbone. Application to the 50-atom-truncated GEOM-Drugs dataset yields lower XVR while the per-bond and per-angle quality is retained. These results position ADT as a practical proposer for in silico molecular design.

</details>


### [320] [Contrastive learning of dynamical representations for enhanced molecular sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15495)
*Kai Zhu,Jintu Zhang,Pietro Novelli,Tingjun Hou,Luigi Bonati*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: SelfTICA是一个自监督对比学习框架，通过将集体变量发现重构为动态表示学习，从时间延迟的分子构型中学习可复用的慢动态模式，无需预定义亚稳态或高质量动力学训练轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法识别集体变量（CV）时，往往需要预定义亚稳态、精心选择的描述符或具有高质量动力学信息的训练轨迹，限制了其在实际复杂系统中的应用。

Method: SelfTICA采用自监督对比学习框架：从时间延迟的分子构型中定义正负样本对，通过对比损失（链接谱变分原理）学习动态表示，再在学到的表示空间应用时间滞后独立成分分析（TICA）提取正交慢模。表示学习与慢模提取解耦，支持在不同滞后时间下评估谱和CV而无需重新训练。

Result: 在多种原子系统中，SelfTICA能从有限的、有偏的或探索性数据中学习动态表示，并转化为有效的集体变量，加速稀有事件探索并改善自由能收敛。

Conclusion: SelfTICA通过将表示学习与慢模提取解耦，避免了直接优化基于特征分解的目标，提供了一种灵活、可复用的集体变量发现方法，适用于数据质量受限的实际场景。

Abstract: Identifying collective variables that capture slow dynamical modes is essential for sampling rare events in complex systems. Existing machine-learning approaches often require predefined metastable states, carefully chosen descriptors, or training trajectories with high-quality kinetic information. Here, we introduce SelfTICA, a self-supervised contrastive-learning framework that reformulates collective-variable discovery as dynamical representation learning. SelfTICA defines positive and negative pairs from time-lagged molecular configurations, learns reusable features through a contrastive objective linked to spectral variational principles, and extracts orthogonal slow modes by applying time-lagged independent component analysis in the learned representation space. By decoupling representation learning from slow-mode extraction, SelfTICA avoids direct optimization of eigendecomposition-based objectives and enables spectra and collective variables to be evaluated across lag times without retraining. Across different atomistic systems, SelfTICA learns dynamical representations from limited, biased, or exploratory data and converts them into collective variables that accelerate rare-event exploration and improve free-energy convergence.

</details>
